In his prayers to the spiritual master, Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura confirms that all the revealed scriptures accept the spiritual master to be identical with the Personality of Godhead because he is a very dear and confidential servant of the Lord. Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas therefore worship Śrīla Gurudeva (the spiritual master) in the light of his being the servitor of the Personality of Godhead. In all the ancient literatures of devotional service and in the more recent songs of Śrīla Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, and other unalloyed Vaiṣṇavas, the spiritual master is always considered to be either one of the confidential associates of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī or a manifested representation of Śrīla Nityānanda Prabhu.”

In the *Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad* (1.2.12) it is said that Brahman, or the Absolute Truth, is comprehended by *jñāna* alone and not by *karma*.

*parīkṣya lokān karma-citān brāhmaṇo*  
*nirvedam āyān nāsty akṛtaḥ kṛtena*  
*tad-vijñānārthaṁ sa gurum evābhigacchet*  
*samit-pāṇiḥśrotriyaṁ brahma-niṣṭham*

**Let a** ***brāhmaṇa,*** **after he has examined all these worlds, which are gained by works (*****karma*****), acquire freedom from all desires. Nothing that is eternal (not made) can be gained by that which is not eternal (made). To understand this, let him take firewood in his hand and approach a guru who is learned and dwells entirely in Brahman.**

It is clear from this that a person who doesn’t entirely dwell in Brahman, who is not *brahma-niṣṭha,* cannot deliver the truth to his disciple.

Lord Caitanya is *jagad-guru*, yet it was by taking position of a disciple that He expertly demonstrated the duty of the guru (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā* 7.71–74):

*prabhu kahe—śuna, śrīpāda, ihāra kāraṇa*  
*guru more mūrkha dekhi’ karila śāsana*

**Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu replied \[to Prakāśānanda Sarasvatī, who had asked Him why, as a** ***sannyāsī,*** **He was dancing and chanting instead of hearing the** ***Vedānta-sūtra*** **and meditating:\] “My dear sir, kindly hear the reason. My spiritual master considered Me a fool, and therefore he chastised Me.**

*mūrkha tumi, tomāra nāhika vedāntādhikāra*  
*‘kṛṣṇa-mantra’ japa sadā,—ei mantra-sāra*

**“You are a fool,’ he said. ‘You are not qualified to study Vedānta philosophy, and therefore You must always chant the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. This is the essence of all mantras, or Vedic hymns.**

*kṛṣṇa-mantra haite habe saṁsāra-mocana*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma haite pābe kṛṣṇera caraṇa*

**“Simply by chanting the holy name of Kṛṣṇa one can obtain freedom from material existence. Indeed, simply by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra one will be able to see the lotus feet of the Lord.**

*nāma vinu kali-kāle nāhi āra dharma*  
*sarva-mantra-sāra nāma, ei śāstra-marma*

**“In this Age of Kali there is no religious principle other than the chanting of the holy name, which is the essence of all Vedic hymns. This is the purport of all scriptures.’”**

Lord Caitanya fearlessly cut out all the red tape of spiritual life by saying “My guru saw Me as a fool.” Actually, one who chants Hare Kṛṣṇa in Kali-yuga is the only intelligent person (*sumedhā*). From *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi-līlā*3.77–78):

*saṅkīrtana-pravartaka śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya*  
*saṅkīrtana-yajñe tāṅre bhaje, sei dhanya*

**Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya is the initiator of** ***saṅkīrtana*** **\[congregational chanting of the holy name of the Lord\]. One who worships Him through** ***saṅkīrtana*** **is fortunate indeed.**

*sei ta’ sumedhā, āra kubuddhi saṁsāra*  
*sarva-yajña haite kṛṣṇa-nāma-yajña sāra*

**Such a person is truly intelligent, whereas others, who have but a poor fund of knowledge, must endure the cycle of repeated birth and death. Of all sacrificial performances, the chanting of the Lord’s holy name is the most sublime.**

In *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya-līlā* 11.99) Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭācārya says to King Pratāparudra:

*saṅkīrtana-yajñe tāṅre kare ārādhana*  
*sei ta’ sumedhā, āra—kali-hata-jana*

**Anyone who worships Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu by congregational chanting should be understood to be very intelligent. One who does not do so must be considered a victim of this age and bereft of all intelligence.**

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s dealings with Prakāśānanda provide the ideal example that you followed in establishing your mission. You preached humbly but firmly.

Factually, anyone who wastes his time studying the *Vedānta-sūtra* or anything else but Vaiṣṇava scriptures is a fool, a victim of the Age of Kali. But the Lord presented himself as a fool to show how a preacher should humbly win the hearts of his powerful and learned opponents. That is why you tolerated all the bad habits of your disciples and gave so much importance to persons such as Allen Ginsberg, even though they were untouchable according to any spiritual standard.

You were so lenient with us, and yet because of your own personal purity and strength, Kṛṣṇa made us strong and determined to help you preach and establish the Lord’s mission. Everything was done by the Lord to please His pure devotee, but we think we are the doers and that is why we have so much difficulty in your absence.

You never felt you had to go anywhere for personal association after your spiritual master entered *samādhi*, because you always felt Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta was by your side. And that was because you sincerely tried to preach as per his instructions. You dedicated your mind, body, and intelligence to fulfilling his instructions. Thus you never missed his association or lacked in faith. So actually the solution of all our problems is simple: We should always remain fools in front of you and simply follow in your footsteps.

I remember how I wrote to you in the Centennial Vyāsa-pūjā book, asking you to bless us so we could finish the Ahmedabad temple construction and be able to offer it to you during the first six months of 1997. It is only by your divine grace that we succeeded and a beautiful new Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda mandir opened. Now again we beg your blessings so that we can realize our dream of offering you a beautiful Kṛṣṇa conscious diorama and multimedia museum. Please bless us so we can complete this project in the next two years. Then during 2009, or at latest during 2010, we may offer you this theistic exhibition, which will be unique not only in Gujarat or India but in the whole world.

Only you can do it.

*kṛṣṇa se tomāra kṛṣṇa dite pāra*  
*tomāra śakati āche*  
*āmi to ‘kāṅgāla ‘kṛṣṇa’ ‘kṛṣṇa’ boli’*  
*dhāi tava pāche pāche*

**Kṛṣṇa is yours; you have the power to give Him to me. I am simply running behind you, shouting “Kṛṣṇa! Kṛṣṇa!”**

You taught us to pray to Kṛṣṇa: “My dear Lord, if you so desire, please bless us to engage in this service.” So we humbly ask you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, if you so desire, please let us offer you with love and devotion a wonderful Kṛṣṇa conscious exhibition.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Ahmedabad, India.  
(written by Yaśomatīnandana Dāsa)

## Aravade

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on your auspicious appearance day!

It’s amazing that on the auspicious day of Nandotsava you appeared in this material world to deliver fallen souls like us. It was predicted by the astrologers that you would travel all over the world and preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Caitanya Mahāprabhu predicted that the holy name would be spread all over the world. You have made the prediction of Mahāprabhu come true.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is very sad that your senior disciples are leaving this world. One by one, they are departing. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so that we will be able to bear your absence and your disciples’ absence, and that we will continue to follow your instructions very strictly, as you desired.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we read from your letters that you wanted to make the devotees very serious in their Kṛṣṇa consciousness by boiling the milk. In Aravade there are now more than twenty full-time devotees, and more are willing to join. We are doing our best to train them nicely, to encourage them to follow your rules and regulations very strictly.

Now our new temple is coming up. Construction is going on very fast. When it is completed, many people from the surrounding area will come and take your *darśana* and the *darśana* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāla and become Kṛṣṇa conscious.

You have given us everything. All we have to do is follow in your footsteps. The scriptures explain that we have to follow in the footsteps of the previous *ācāryas*. You are not only our previous *ācārya* but also the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON. ISKCON is yours, and you are ISKCON’s. Please allow us to stay here to please you and develop our love for Kṛṣṇa.

Thank you.

The devotees at ISKCON Aravade, India.  
(written by Dharmarāja Dāsa)

## Athens

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!In *Śrī Caitanya-bhāgavata* (*Ādi-khaṇḍa* 8.151–52), we read:

*ananta brahmāṇḍa yāṅra loma-kūpe bhāse*  
*sṛṣṭi-sthiti-pralaya karaye yāṅra dāse*

*brahmā-śiva-ādi matta yāṅra guṇa-dhyāne*  
*hena-prabhu nidrā yāna śacīra aṅgane*

**He whose hair pores are the source of innumerable universes, He whose servants direct the creation, maintenance, and destruction of those universes, He whose glories intoxicate the minds of even Śiva and Brahmā — that same Lord was now sleeping in the courtyard of Śrīmatī Śacī devī.**

Thank you very much for giving us the opportunity to participate in the preaching mission of Lord Caitanya. Our prayer is to always serve you and become more and more dedicated to you. Just thinking of your divine qualities helps us overcome any adversity. We beg to become instruments in your hands in order to intoxicate Greece with Lord Gaurāṅga’s mercy — now.

Your servants in Athens, Greece.  
(written by Nāma-saṅkīrtana Dāsa)

## Austin

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer you our eternal obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

We are here in Austin, Texas, trying to spread your mission. It was here in the spring of 1971 that His Holiness Viṣṇujana Mahārāja came to introduce ISKCON to the deluded souls of Austin. He was an amazingly empowered personality. Even though he came alone with no money, within several weeks he had a temple building full of devotees. Many, many souls who came in contact with him became uplifted and inspired to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. Since that day when Viṣṇujana Mahārāja brought your mission here, Austin has never been the same. It is now a pastime place of the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are begging your mercy that you will empower us in the same way that Viṣṇujana Mahārāja was empowered, so that we can attract many, many souls to dedicate their lives to that Supreme All-Attractive Personality, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

The servants of your servants at ISKCON Austin, Texas, USA.

## Baha (Bali)

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We devotees from Bali, Indonesia, offer our most humble and respectful obeisances. Please accept our offering in honor of this most blessed event — your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā.

The worst flood in years hit the Indonesian capital of Jakarta in February 2007, killing at least 94 people. More than 70,000 houses were destroyed and 200,000 people displaced. But there is more to come: A United Nations–backed panel recently gave a report on the impact of global warming on human life. The report portrays a bleak future for the world’s poorest countries, which will be hit hardest. Because of rising sea levels, low-lying regions will be flooded, driving millions inland. In Bangladesh and Vietnam, a predicted one-meter rise in sea levels will displace over 40 million people. It is also expected that higher global temperatures will lead to changing crop yields, increasing famine and disease. Drought will become more frequent as temperatures rise. Last year drought threatened the lives and livelihoods of over 10 million people in Ethiopia and Kenya.

By now it is proven beyond doubt that the blame for these climatic changes is squarely on manmade emissions. For decades, the inhabitants of the Western world have dealt carelessly and wastefully with resources. These countries are largely responsible for the constant rise of carbon dioxide emissions. Unless these emissions drop sharply, there will be up to a six-degree increase in the global temperature. This is four degrees above what is seen as allowable. Recent economic ambitions of newly industrializing countries like China and India make it very unlikely that such a reduction will take place, despite the effort of some committed countries.

“Is a crisis necessary to bring people to a new path or can they react to a gradual growth in knowledge?” Ralph Cicerone, president of the US National Academy of Sciences, asked after the unveiling of new report. What is required that the world’s citizens change their behavior? Is the scientist’s conjuring of a climate apocalypse in the near future sufficient to awaken people?

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, you openly declare in your commentary to the *Bhagavad-gītā* that materialistic people have no idea how to behave and that therefore they are to be seen as the enemies of the world. You say that they will create a situation which will bring destruction to all (*Bhagavad-gītā* 16.9). If they do not come to their senses very soon, your forecast will manifest.

By your appearance on this planet and the establishment of your ISKCON, there is now hope. At last we have the answers. You give mankind the much-needed practical directions for simpler living and the pursuit of nonmaterial satisfaction. There is no other solution to our planet’s environment crisis. It is the sole valid course to change the suicide path of the planet’s history for good. It is hard to estimate how soon your spiritual message will affect the materialistic people. For now we can only see that many new devotees are joining your mission. We, your longstanding servants here in Bali and the many that have recently joined, are all very grateful that you have come to take us away from blind leaders who direct us to the erroneous path of animalistic sense gratification. We feel obliged to you and those newcomers. We take it as our responsibility to properly take shelter of you by studying your instructions and giving shelter to others. For this reason we started a Bhakti-śāstrī course, with thirty English-speaking devotees taking part. These students will in turn become teachers of the same course in their local languages. Your books contain everything one needs to know to get the spiritual strength to overcome the urges of the senses and the mind. By your formula, which you termed “routine work,” one is able to step out of the madness of this modern civilization. That is our experience and it works for everyone else too. There is no better method.

People without your kind guidance will go on debating about solutions for the planet-in-crisis, trying for friendly types of energies, etc. But no form of energy will direct the temperature curve of the planet downward without consumers rethinking their way of life. Life is meant for God realization and not sense gratification. You said this many times, and any thoughtful person can increasingly see how this must be true; otherwise why the ever-increasing problems with no solutions, despite all the panels and reports that try to solve the crisis?

*Yajña* must be performed, for otherwise people will be entangled in sinful activities and will suffer immensely. Therefore the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement has taken charge of introducing the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa all over the world. . . . In conclusion, therefore, in this Age of Kali people all over the world should refrain from the four principles of sinful life — illicit sex, meat-eating, intoxication and gambling — and in a pure state of existence should perform the simple *yajña* of chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. Then the earth will certainly produce all the necessities for life, and people will be happy economically, politically, socially, religiously, and culturally. Everything will be in proper order. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 9.20.26\]

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, in a purport of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (2.8.6) you describe a pure devotee:

\[A\] devotee of the Lord cannot give up the service of the Lord, for he is ordered by his spiritual master. Pure devotees like Nārada and Nityānanda Prabhu take up the order of the spiritual master as the sustenance of life. They do not mind what becomes of the future of their lives. They take the matter very seriously, as the order comes from the higher authority — from the representative of the Lord or from the Lord Himself.

We pray to you for your blessings so that we can progress on this spiritual path and become pure devotees who “do not mind what becomes of the future of \[our\] lives.” We do not want to worry about floods and other disturbances. What we really want is to become pure devotees so that our lives will be full of yearning to please you. We have some understanding of what pleases you: The distribution of your books and when devotees learn to represent your teachings in their own words. With your blessing we will do so. We are maintained by your mercy. Our unworthy obeisances are at your lotus feet.

Your servants at ISKCON Bali, Indonesia.

## Baltimore

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.**

**mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim**  
**yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam**

**I offer my most respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of all fallen souls, whose mercy can turn the dumb into eloquent speakers and enable the lame to cross mountains.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I am writing this offering on behalf of the devotee community of New Kulīna-grāma Dhāma. That is why in the above two prayers “I” has been used. We devotees in this temple are not your direct disciples, yet the “my spiritual master” phrase has been used. The reason is that you are a *jagad-guru*--, a spiritual master of the entire world, and that you are the *śikṣā-guru* of all devotees in ISKCON, as well as everyone else who gets guidance from your books. So even though we are your spiritual grandchildren, we may address you as our spiritual master. As mentioned in the above *śloka,* by your mercy Kṛṣṇa gives even a babbling idiot like me an opportunity to praise Your Divine Grace on this very auspicious day of your divine appearance.

Our temple, New Kulīna-grāma Dhāma, was blessed by the touch of your lotus feet when you visited this temple on July 7, 1976. Mahārāja Yudhiṣṭhira praised Vidura by saying:

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni svāntaḥ-sthena gadābhṛtā*

**My Lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage. \[*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.13.10\]**

Similarly, our temple has become a *tīrtha* for all, including future generations of devotees. There are many temples in America, but not every one has been visited by Your Divine Grace. Therefore our temple, even though a very old building, is very precious to us. When you saw the photograph of our most beautiful presiding Deities here in Baltimore, Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara, you were immediately attracted to them, so much so that you wanted to take their *darśana* right away. You could not do so that very same day, but you arrived the next morning to turn an ordinary place into a place of pilgrimage for all Vaiṣṇavas. You spoke on two verses from *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, *Madhya-līlā* 20.100 and 20.102, which recount the conversation between Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī and Lord Śrī Caitanya where Sanātana Gosvāmī says:

*āpanāra hitāhita kichui nā jāni!*  
*grāmya-vyavahāre paṇḍita, tāi satya māni*

**I do not know what is beneficial for me or what is detrimental. Nonetheless, in ordinary dealings people consider me a learned scholar, and I am also thinking of myself as such.**

*‘ke āmi’, ‘kene āmāya jāre tāpa-traya’*  
*ihā nāhi jāni—‘kemane hita haya’*

**Who am I? Why do the threefold miseries always give me trouble? If I do not know this, how can I be benefited?**

You were so much pleased that day that you instructed all the devotees present that by chanting “*Jaya* Śacīnandana*,* Gaurahari!” and dancing in front of the Deities everyone will go back to Godhead. This is the promise you gave to the devotees, and Lord Kṛṣṇa always fulfills the desire of His pure devotee. With that faith and conviction this lowly, insignificant granddisciple servant is begging you to bless us so that we can execute your instructions wholeheartedly.

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura writes in his *Śaraṇāgati:*

*ekākīāmāra, nāhi pāya bala,*  
*hari-nāma-saṅkīrtane*  
*tumi kṛpā kori’, śraddhā-bindu diyā,*  
*deho’ kṛṣṇa-nāma-dhane*

**On my own, I find I have no strength to chant the holy name of Lord Hari. Please be merciful by awarding me a particle of faith and giving me the great treasure of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.**

Please give us the strength to respect every living entity so that we can chant the holy name of Lord Gaurāṅga in a humble state of mind. You have empowered many devotees to perform Herculean tasks. This is all because of your staunch faith in your spiritual master’s words and in the power of the holy name.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, after you brought Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the Western world, many pseudo religions have followed and are flourishing very well under the influence of the Age of Kali. Their pomp attracts many common people. Śrīla Śukadeva Gosvāmī predicted the flourishing of pseudo religions while describing King Arhat, who heard of the activities of Lord Ṛṣabhadeva and, imitating His principles, introduced a new system of religion.

In any case, how many people can purchase a real diamond? Please guide us on the right path to the genuine diamond, rather than some cheap imitation. We believe that, by your blessing, sincerity in our purpose will lead to success.

Your servants at New Kulīna-grāma Dhāma, Baltimore, Maryland, USA.  
(written by Jaya Gaurasundara Dāsa)

## Baroda

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Although we can never respect you enough by any means, please accept our humble and most sincere prostrated obeisances at your divine lotus feet, which are the boat for crossing over the ocean of nescience.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa says to Uddhava in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 11.29.34:

*martyo yadā tyakta-samasta-karmā*  
*niveditātmā vicikīrṣito me*  
*tadāmṛtatvaṁ pratipadyamāno*  
*mayātma-bhūyāya ca kalpate vai*

**A person who gives up all fruitive activities and offers himself entirely unto Me, eagerly desiring to render service unto Me, achieves liberation from birth and death and is promoted to the status of sharing My own opulences.**

Certainly you were free from all fruitive efforts, and thus you gave yourself fully to the service of the Lord, and from your behavior, activities, and qualities it is not at all difficult to infer that you were *jīvan-mukta*. Service to Guru and Gaurāṅga was the only goal of your life, and in order to execute the instructions of Guru and Gaurāṅga, you never hesitated in being daring and active. Your compassion for the fallen, conditioned souls is unparalleled and unprecedented. To fulfill the desire of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, you always took extraordinary risks and trials, which are impossible for the man of uncontrolled senses. Obviously, because of your hundred-percent commitment to your beloved *gurudeva,* you were his dearmost disciple and thus received his full mercy. As described above, a pure devotee shares the divine opulences of Kṛṣṇa. By studying your biography, we can appreciate how you were qualified to share Kṛṣṇa’s divine opulences, namely beauty, knowledge, wealth, strength, fame, and renunciation. A man or woman of *jaḍa-buddhi* cannot understand how you share Kṛṣṇa’s opulences, but your sincere followers are certainly able to understand this by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. Being such an elevated soul, you were always humble, unlike the person who becomes puffed-up even by possessing the temporary stoollike opulences of this world.

Dear Prabhupāda, kindly always engage us in the service of your divine lotus feet and thus turn us into *kṛṣṇa-mati* from *jaḍa-mati*. Kindly always give us the opportunity and inspiration to serve your movement by whatever means you like. Let our will be dovetailed with yours. Let service to your divine feet take priority over our personal ambitions and personal pride and prejudice. Kindly enable us to eternally engage in serving Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

Your eternal servants at ISKCON Baroda, India.  
(written by Jaya Dāmodara Dāsa)

## New Jagannatha Puri (Berkeley)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace, savior of the whole world!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we fallen followers of yours in your New Jagannātha Purī Dhāma are happily remembering you during this special year, the 111th anniversary of your all-auspicious appearance. This is the fortieth anniversary of (1) your first visit to the western United States (to San Francisco), (2) the first establishment of Deities in an ISKCON temple (Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Śrīmatī Subhadrā Devī here in New Jagannātha Purī), and (3) your first Ratha-yātrā festival in the Western world, held in 1967 and every single year since then in San Francisco. This is also the fortieth anniversary of the initiation of your beloved disciple Jayānanda Prabhu, our perfect exemplar and favorite temple president, and the thirtieth anniversary of his and your disappearance from our external vision.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, who can conceive of your kindness? Who can imagine mercy like yours, mercy that overlooks all our woeful inadequacies and goes on giving growing guidance even three decades after your divine disappearance? We are lazy and have no intelligence or determination. Yet somehow or other, amazing things are happening all around us by your grace. Thousands of your books are being distributed here every month. Hundreds of guests come every Sunday to your sumptuous Love Feasts with its ecstatic *kīrtanas*. Each and every Saturday afternoon faithful Lokanātha Prabhu takes a *harināma* party up fortunate Telegraph Avenue to the University of California campus. Every morning and evening your Deities here, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, along with your first ISKCON Deities, look absolutely gorgeous as they bless with Their *darśana* and Their *prasādam* many dozens of devotees and visitors — anyone who comes. Who could hope for such a healthy home or ask for such an auspicious atmosphere?

Most wonderful of all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the new Weekend Warrior program: congregational and temple devotees, inspired by Vaiśeṣika Prabhu and led by Mother Lalitā, go out one weekend every month to distribute your books and Lord Jagannātha’s *prasādam*. Absolutely astonishing is Rasarāja Prabhu’s profound scientific preaching: He explains to intellectuals, including leading theoretical physicists of the world, how some of the most intractable problems of modern science can be solved by a new scientific paradigm based on Your Divine Grace’s teachings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your New Jagannātha Purī community has struggled for years to serve you better each day. We have had our ups and downs, but just this year something special has happened, reminding us how you are always present with us, supervising and protecting our community: Your sincere servants Romapāda Swami and Nityānanda Prabhu, President and Secretary of the Executive Committee of your North American GBC and Temple Presidents, came here and gained a clear understanding of our problems. Their honesty, perceptiveness, and competence have convinced us that, through your faithful followers like them, you are continuing to carefully monitor your movement and weed out the deviations we foolishly allow to develop within it. We feel that, just as you did forty years ago on your first visit here to your fledgling hippie disciples, you are still with us, inspiring us through special mercy in the form of Romapāda Mahārāja and Nityānanda Prabhu’s visit, to ever increase our service to your New Jagannātha Purī temple.

Through these two intelligent devotees of yours, and through so many others like Vaiśeṣika Prabhu and Aditi Mātājī, you are deepening our understanding of your instructions and helping us understand more and more how essential it is that we work together closely and cooperatively as Vaiṣṇavas, appreciating one another’s service. You are especially reminding us that we must always focus our attention on hearing and studying your books daily and distributing them as widely as we can and in better and more intelligent ways, being careful to always leave everyone we meet with a good impression, for we are your representatives.

We will never be able to do what we should for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but we will never stop trying. By the causeless compassion and ever-increasing inspiration of Your Divine Grace, as well as of your devoted disciples who once blessed us here in New Jagannātha Purī but have since transferred to your “ISKCON in the spiritual world” (as you called it), -like Viṣṇujana Mahārāja, our own Jayānanda Prabhu, Gaura-govinda Mahārāja, Tribhuvaneśvarī Mātājī, Upendra Prabhu, Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami, Śrīdhara Swami, Bhakti-tīrtha Swami, Bhakti Svarūpa Dāmodara Mahārāja, and so many others, we will forever go on serving your mission the best we can. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your eternal servants in New Jagannātha Purī Dhāma, ISKCON Berkeley, California, USA.  
(written by Amoghalīlā Dāsa)

## Bhaktivedanta Manor

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, not only does Vyāsa-pūjā celebrate your amazing achievements, but it gives us an opportunity to glorify the principle of *paramparā*. Only by following your instructions via the disciplic succession can we please the Lord, and only through His help can we have any positive results to offer.

The Manor community has been expanding for almost thirty-five years. The estate has grown from the original seventeen acres to over ninety. The extra land has been used according to your instructions. We now have forty cows and bulls, with more to come. We are awaiting permission to build a new goshala, and all the temple buildings have been refurbished, including new roof-tiling. All this is completed, and all bills have been settled.

We have the largest Indian community of any ISKCON temple outside India. Through intelligent preaching over the years, the financial base of the Manor is no longer dependent on the book distributors. In the office of the temple president you will find pictures of the British Prime Minister and even the Queen conversing with the devotees. Every day during the weeks leading up to the Janmāṣṭamī festival, more than three hundred volunteers work late into the evening. They set up all the tents and displays. Every night in the kitchen tent, scores of devotees prepare *prasādam* to feed the volunteers. The festival plays host to over seventy thousand visitors. Considering the sheer scale of Janmāṣṭamī, it is even more remarkable that these same devotees and volunteers are up early the following day cleaning and preparing for the Vyāsa-pūjā celebration. Since I have been here, I have always been inspired by the dignity and respect shown toward Your Divine Grace. Your rooms here at the Manor have been preserved as a museum. They are kept immaculate and are visited all year round. The rooms also provide a setting for special classes and seminars.

During the school term, the Manor hosts visiting schoolchildren as part of their religious education studies. Over the course of a year, thousands of children get their first *darśana* of Lord Kṛṣṇa and take *prasādam*.

Devotees have two book marathons each year. The traditional Christmas marathon goes on in much the same way as when you were physically present. There is also a summer book marathon. As an old *saṅkīrtana* devotee myself, I am particularly happy to see the same magic coming through the younger generations. In fact, both here and at the Soho Temple in downtown London there are men and ladies who can be described as “the real deal.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught us to use these temples as a place to attract the conditioned souls to Lord Kṛṣṇa. From the moment one drives through the front gates of the Manor, it is obvious that someone very, very special lives here. The gardens are maintained beautifully, and the cows are grazing happily, just as in Vṛndāvana.

As one makes his way into the inner rooms of the building, one begins to feel the presence of someone far greater than those people one ordinarily meets. Then . . . there They are, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda! The most wonderful Transcendental Couple, whom you personally installed in 1973! We are also graced by Their Lordships Śrī ŚrīSītā-Rāma and, in the lower middle of the altar, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

One of my favorite activities is chanting *japa* at the back of the temple room and watching the reaction of the various guests as they behold the altar.

A hundred yards away from the main building one sees two state-of-the-art greenhouses. Inside are more than eighty of the most healthy Tulasī plants one could find anywhere. They are all overseen by a Deity of Śrī Vṛndā Devī herself. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you told us that the spiritual health of the temple could be judged by the health and worship of Śrīmatī Tulasī Devī. I am pleased to report that Bhaktivedanta Manor is in very good health.

The Manor also has a primary school teaching approximately twenty-five devotee children. The teachers work very hard to insure that we meet the educational standards the government has set. But for the students it is the devotional relationships that are forged in the early years of their lives that will prove most valuable.

Recently, a senior godsister was visiting the Manor. She commented on how vibrant and busy the temple was and that so many different preaching programs were taking place. In this year’s offering I have tried to give an overview of what takes place here at Bhaktivedanta Manor. I often think of the Manor community as resembling a beehive. Everything done on the grounds and gardens is for the pleasure of Their Lordships. When we travel away from the temple to the surrounding towns, we see devotees everywhere. We see them at the supermarket or getting petrol for their cars; we find them walking down the road. One loud shout of “Haribol!” and their heads turn around. Sometimes, as I stand near the entrance gate, I watch cars turn in from all directions. Just like honeybees, they are all in search of nectar. They are drawn toward the lotus feet of the Lord. Just as there is a seemingly endless stream of cars going into the temple, there are all kinds of vehicles coming out. They are full of devotees armed with books and *prasādam*.

When I was invited to write this year’s offering on behalf of Bhaktivedanta Manor, I decided it should be a snapshot of the community and its achievements. I didn’t seek any praise for myself. Quite the opposite. All these activities simply glorify how your mission is continuing since your departure. I could not thank the hundreds of volunteers had they not been engaged in devotional service by your granddisciples. I could not thank those granddisciples had they not been trained properly by your direct disciples, my godbrothers. Finally, who among us would have even heard about Lord Kṛṣṇa had it not been for Your Divine Grace? Śrīla Prabhupāda, we all want to shower you with the credit you deserve. Your position within the *paramparā* will always be as the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON. You are the diamond link on a golden chain to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor, UK.  
(written by Sañjaya Dāsa)

## Brahmapur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

By your mercy and your desire to see Kṛṣṇa consciousness preached in every town and village, we have opened a preaching center here in Brahmapur, a coastal city in Orissa with a population of about 300,000 pious people. It is a five-hour bus ride from Bhubaneswar, the capital of Orissa.

To have the *darśana* of Lord Kṛṣṇa, people here have to go all the way to Bhubaneswar or Purī. Most of the poor people here cannot afford the time or money to go to those far-off places to worship the Lord. So they were craving a Hare Kṛṣṇa temple in Brahmapur for a very long time.

Perhaps in answer to their prayers, the Lord has arranged to manifest a temple here to bless these innocent people. By the Lord’s arrangement, a charitable Kṛṣṇa devotee named Shri H.B. Sahu, a retired Marine Officer of Brahmapur, came forward to donate his land, measuring about 15,360 sq. ft. and costing more than two million rupees. Behind the land is a small pond, which provides plenty of water; the site is most suitable for a temple. This place seems chosen by Lord Kṛṣṇa himself, as indicated by the fact that many cows have come to the nearby land to graze and to drink water from the well dug for temple use. A few days ago the land was transferred to ISKCON’s name without any problem.

Once the temple is built, the place will become a very important tourist center in this region, being very close to the national highways. Many devotees living nearby are expected to visit the temple daily.

His Grace Bhīma Prabhu, Western Zonal Secretary and B.B.T. Trustee based in Mumbai, was kind enough to give a liberal donation to start the construction. The worshipable Deities for the temple have arrived from Jaipur. Now the work is progressing for a temporary temple for the Deities, and with the grace of Kṛṣṇa a small but graceful temple will be opened in three or four years. For now we will continue as a preaching center.

One of the top priorities of this temple will be book distribution, to satisfy your wish, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Simultaneously, congregational preaching and devotee cultivation will be expanded to make this temple one of the most popular in this state.

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, we rededicate this preaching center to your lotus feet, and we promise that we at this center and the soon-to-be temple will carefully follow your teachings in promoting Kṛṣṇa consciousness among the people here, whom we all regard as our godbrothers and -sisters.

Your servants at ISKCON Brahmapur, Orissa, India.  
(written by Pañcaratna Dāsa)

## Brisbane

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, your disciples and granddisciples, bow down together at your lotus feet to offer you our most humble obeisances.

When we bow down to you, Your Divine Grace, we are showing our surrender to you. By surrendering to you, we are surrendering to Lord Kṛṣṇa. By surrendering to you, we are trying to do what pleases you. In the line of duty one must not only perform acts which one likes, but also one must perform acts which one does not like. That is real surrender.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Brisbane is such an unfortunate place because you never came here, even though you visited Australia four times. But it is so fortunate since, by your causeless mercy, we have a small Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi *mandira* and a small but very cohesive devotee community. We were really struggling a few years ago, but because of your precious gifts of the holy name, your books, your disciples, and your granddisciples, we have started to take baby steps again. We hope that one day we will spread Lord Caitanya’s mission in every town and village, from Coffs Harbour to Cairns, and even in the interiors of the land.

It is really impossible to glorify Your Divine Grace enough. The gifts you gave us have made our lives sublime: your books, your devotees, the holy name, sweet and melodious *kīrtanas*, simply wonderful *prasādam,* and above all the highest philosophy. I heard in 2005, while on the Polish *yātrā*, that others can give us something but only you and your ISKCON can give us the highest — *vraja-bhakti* of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. You made that difficult task so simple — just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy! This was your matchless gift.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only for your pleasure that the sincere devotees in our *yātrā* are carrying out your orders. We distribute *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* in two restaurants and cater programs at almost all the major public festivals (our catering programs are said to be the best in Australia), distribute your books, preach through Saturday and Sunday love feasts, preach privately through home Bhakti-vṛkṣa and Bhakti-yoga programs, maintain nice Deity worship, and celebrate devotional festivals for the pleasure of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. We hope that are happy to watch us take these tiny baby steps toward spreading your mission.

As you always said, material life is always full of struggle; but devotees also have their fair share of struggle. Since Brisbane is a tropical sleepy paradise, we are struggling to fight Māyā, who appears in so many forms. On a working day we can hardly ever attend the temple programs. Sometimes we struggle during the festivals to present bigger and better programs. But above all, please give us strength to take care of our existing devotees while we make new devotees. Please give us your blessings so that we can be really personal with the devotees; not that we preach personalism and be impersonal with our own family of devotees.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, as a child and a grandchild have the right to ask gifts from the father and grandfather, we at the Brisbane *yātrā* ask you for the following blessings:

- That we seriously study your books and distribute them profusely.
- That we care about each other and inspire one another to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.
- That we ourselves become pure by attentively chanting the holy names and distributing this reservoir of pleasure to everyone.
- That we carry out all your orders co-operatively and in the right spirit.

Above all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so that we can follow your very high standards in a co-operative mood, so that our children, their children, and the generations to come will get the nectar of your pure loving nature.

Every morning we pray to Their Lordships, “Dear Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, if You so desire, please allow us to build a new temple for Your pleasure.”Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the understanding by which we can differentiate between our inabilities and the Lord’s desires, and give us the strength to accept the inevitable.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, once when asked whether you treated any of your disciples specially, His Grace Śrutakīrti Prabhu replied, “Yes, all of them.”

To conclude, as Prahlāda Mahārāja says to Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva in the Seventh Canto, we would like to say to you, “O my lord, I am your unmotivated servant, and you are my eternal master. There is no need of our being anything other than master and servant. You are naturally my master, and I am naturally your servant. We have no other relationship.”

Your eternal spiritual children and grandchildren at ISKCON Brisbane, Australia.

## Budapest

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you!

I write you in the name of the devotees of the Budapest temple in Hungary, a small country in the middle of Europe. While you were present on this planet you did not personally visit this country, but your disciples fortunately did. As a result of their wonderful and self-sacrificing work, there is now a little oasis in the desert of the darkest ignorance.

Last year in Budapest we managed to sink a well in the most dangerous and driest part of the desert. We had been looking for the proper place for a long time, but your dear disciples and sons helped us find the right location. We wanted a place with which you would be satisfied entirely. Since you are undoubtedly present in the devotees’ hearts, we felt relief that we made the right choice when everybody liked the place and the plans.

Since then more guests have paid their respects to this temple and confirmed: you would be satisfied with this place. Of course they look at not only the building but also the devotion of the wonderful devotees who serve here. Your nice daughter Lakṣmīmonī Mātājī visited us and said in her lecture that she sees that the devotees here try to follow your instructions nicely and strictly. You always emphasized that we should read your books and listen to your lectures; now I have begun to understand why it is so important. This place will be a real oasis only if we keep the well clean. We have to learn to ladle water in such a way that we get only pure water, nothing else. We cannot do it only “by instinct”; we have to follow your simple but perfect instructions strictly. We have to study attentively how you made decisions in certain situations.

Now we have to decide. Often this fact means a heavy weight, but at least it pushes us to pray to you and hold on to your lotus feet. Please give us the strength and intelligence to represent you worthily and not bring shame upon you.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Budapest, Hungary.  
(written by Vijaya Gaurāṅga Dāsa)

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most respected Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine instructions, which are continuing to guide the fallen souls of Kali-yuga, like us, on the path back to Godhead!

We are indebted to you for the transcendental knowledge you provided. Your most precious gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to this world shall ever be cherished by the devout of the day and for ages to come. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the transcendental cloud who showered the nectar of the holy name and saved us from the forest fire of this world. You gave us the most sublime literature, your books, which glorify Kṛṣṇa and establish Him as the Supreme Personality of Godhead. You worked day and night to write these books and shall ever remain manifest through them for the welfare of the whole human civilization.

While standing on a mountain, one will find it difficult to appreciate the mountain’s full size and majesty. One sees only rocks, trees, and water in the immediate field of vision. But if one travels down the mountain slope to the plain and looks back at the mountain, one begins to take in the mountain’s full grandeur. Similarly, with the passage of time, your glories are spreading more and more.

In this material world, if someone knows some business tactics, he won’t teach the same to anyone. A materialistic person wants to be successful himself. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, gave this most confidential knowledge to the whole world and gave everyone a chance to be saved from gliding to a hellish condition of life. How can we thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda? We can never repay you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy we distributed over eleven thousand copies of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* during the last marathon, a record for ISKCON Chandigarh. All devotees (temple, congregation, and ISKCON Youth Forum), inspired by His Holiness Gopāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami, put their heart and soul into achieving this landmark. The devotees went to whatever place was available and distributed your books to remove the darkness of this Age of Kali.

Also, we find that many educated youth are taking inspiration from your books in the city of Chandigarh, which is a center of higher learning. Two young engineers and one recent graduate have joined ISKCON Chandigarh full-time after leaving their lucrative jobs. Our ISKCON Youth Forum is growing steadily, thus bringing more inquisitive young persons to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Furthermore, there has been a significant increase in the number and participation of congregation devotees during the last year, by your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this year we also made a wonderful offering of a beautifully renovated temple hall, with fine acoustics, to your beloved Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava. This temple hall helps us spread the glories of Your Divine Grace and Lord Caitanya. It is a big attraction for the visitors.

Armed with a dedicated team of fired-up young devotees, your highly potent books, and a beautiful *sat-saṅga* hall featuring Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava as the presiding Deities, we plan to bring more educated youth and congregational members under the protection of your divine instructions and increase the distribution of your transcendental books in the coming years, for your pleasure.

For this, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg a particle of the unflinching faith, determination, and enthusiasm you possessed while carrying out the mission of your spiritual master. Please bless us in this way so that we can be successful in our plans to please you.

Begging to remain your eternal servants,  
The devotees at ISKCON Chandigarh, India.

*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Prabhupāda so divine.  
What you did was incredible —  
You gave the world Kṛṣṇa’s love.

## Czech Republic

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I write to you from a small country in the middle of Europe that is called the Czech Republic. You personally never visited this country, but you certainly must have flown above it in a plane on your way to neighboring Germany. Some time ago we found in the VedaBase several of your statements mentioning the Czech Republic, from which we deduce that you thought of us and knew about us.

At that time the totalitarian Communist regime here did not allow anybody to freely practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The Communists were keeping a lid on us here behind the Iron Curtain. But thanks to your mercy and your powerful preaching in the West, things changed. The Iron Curtain cracked, and the flood of the *saṅkīrtana* mission inundated this country as well.

From the time I was small I was educated, like everyone else here, in the bodily conception of life, bemused by the text of our national anthem: “Where my home is, where my home is, . . .” Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, thanks to you we can now understand that our real home is not this Czech Republic but Goloka Vṛndāvana, the eternal abode of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and His associates in the spiritual world.

The anthem continues: “. . . water brawls in the meadows, forests sough in the hills, a flower of spring shines in the orchards, an earthly paradise to all appearance.” Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you exposed this fraud and explained to us that this planet earth is no earthly paradise but Mṛtyu-loka, the planet of death. You taught us to leave this world of death as soon as possible and go back home, back to Godhead.

They used to teach us other stupid songs as well. For instance: “Life is just chance: sometimes you’re up, sometimes you’re down, . . .” Every small child knows this song. But you came to the West to preach just the opposite: “Life does not arise by chance; life comes from life!” You challenged all those scientists and artists, calling them rascals. The song continues: “… life flows like water, and death is like the sea.” What Māyāvāda! The song teaches us that we all merge and lose our individuality. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you came to uproot this impersonalism and voidism from the Western countries and from our hearts — *nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*. We are very grateful to you for that.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am glad that I am not this body and don’t have to eat the “pork with sauerkraut and knõdel.” You revealed to us the holy *dhāma,* with lots of *prasādam*. By your mercy, by your grace, you gave us the higher taste.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the tortoise comes out of the sea, lays its eggs in the sand, goes back to the sea, and then from a distance meditates on the well-being of its offspring. Gradually the offspring emerge from the eggs and instinctively enter the sea, where they meet their loving mother. Similarly, you always meditated on the well-being of the conditioned souls in this material world and always worked hard so that they could emerge from the shell of ignorance and bodily identification, renew their spiritual identity, and return home, to the ocean of the nectar of devotion, to the eternal transcendental pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and His eternal associates.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly allow us to continue to assist you in your preaching mission — to perform our various services nicely and to successfully develop the projects we are working on under the guidance of your disciples. We thank you that in this small country we can study and distribute your books and thus be protected from various pseudo groups of your so-called followers who offer various versions of the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we thank you for all the favor, protection, and shelter you provide us.

The ISKCON devotees in the Czech Republic.  
(written by Priya-kīrti Dāsa)

## Denver

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your most merciful lotus feet.

In trying to find the words to glorify Your Divine Grace, I am feeling most inadequate. Not that there is nothing that can be said or that has not been said. Quite the contrary, just as Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa is forever being glorified for His transcendental qualities and activities, that which is connected to the Supreme Lord, such as His dear devotees, can also be forever glorified. Because their lives are fully surrendered to this service, they are like *cintāmaṇi* stones that can purify the filthiest and most fallen.

My unfortunate position is that I have not taken advantage of those *cintāmaṇi* stones. Instead of surrendering to your lotus feet, I have remained stuck in this miserable mentality of wanting to enjoy. Despite all indicators, namely my failing body, feeble memory, and lethargy, I continue to fantasize, and I ignore inevitable death. Very shortly everything will be taken away, and yet I struggle to hold on to it. I’m just like a donkey that never reaches the hanging carrot but keeps going after it.

Śrī Kavi says to King Nimi (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 11.2.37):

*bhayaṁ dvitīyābhiniveśataḥ syād*  
*īśād apetasya viparyayo ’smṛtiḥ*  
*tan-māyayāto budha ābhajet taṁ*  
*bhaktyaikayeśaṁ guru-devatātmā*

Fear arises when a living entity misidentifies himself as the material body because of absorption in the external, illusory energy of the Lord. When the living entity thus turns away from the Supreme Lord, he also forgets his own constitutional position as a servant of the Lord. This bewildering, fearful condition is effected by the potency for illusion, called *māyā*. Therefore, an intelligent person should engage unflinchingly in the unalloyed devotional service of the Lord, under the guidance of a bona fide spiritual master, whom he should accept as his worshipable deity and as his very life and soul.

Being firmly in the grips of the external energy, I’ve failed to appreciate the great sacrifices you made in reaching out to the suffering souls of this world. Whether writing your books, speaking tirelessly, or teaching by your example, you are truly the *ācārya*. With the help of a few monkey soldiers, you established a worldwide movement for distributing the mercy of the holy name. Being transcendental to all material considerations, you left the beauty of Vṛndāvana for the hell of the West. Surrounded by adoring followers and wealth, yet remaining meek and humble, you exemplified what it means to be a disciple relying solely on the order of the spiritual master. In every way, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our only source of inspiration.

Certainly many things can be said, but so as not to sound too repetitious, we simply pray that you please give your mercy to this unqualified and insignificant servant who has made you his life and soul.

Your servants at ISKCON Denver, Colorado, USA.  
(written by Naikātmā Dāsa)

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

This is the most eagerly awaited day of the year for all the devotees at Devasadan *mandira,* because we receive the nectar of your glories from so many members of our Hare Kṛṣṇa family. Their offerings on this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day show their love for you and their dedication to you and your mission. Just by reading these heartfelt offerings, we realize how amazingly, year after year, you attract so many souls throughout the world to take advantage and participate in pure loving service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Just by reading and hearing the Vyāsa-pūjā offerings from hundreds of your devotees, one can get charged up to do more for your mission, and one realizes how we are all most fortunate to be part of your family of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, the greatest mission in the universe. To bring the ultimate blissful experience, the frontline generals at the BBT compile a collection of precious and rare pictures of your pastimes in the Vyāsa-pūjā book every year. In this way we all get a glimpse of the glorious times when you started spreading the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu throughout the world. Under your guidance and blessings, your movement is growing every day, in every corner of the world, including Detroit.

As you predicted during your visit here when you bought this opulent Fisher Mansion, this neighborhood is now being developed to attract affluent people. Every year our devotees upgrade and maintain the temple building to attract more and more visitors. Students from universities and high schools come regularly to experience and relish first-hand your magnificent establishment of Vedic culture. Your vibrant and energetic team of Bhakti-vṛkṣa devotees take more and more responsibilities in every aspect of the temple. Your Hare Kṛṣṇa Youth, your *gurukulīs,* prove by their vigorous participation that your ISKCON will carry on generation after generation, just as you predicted.

Most merciful Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is you who are bringing this new wave of young, energetic, sincere souls to Devasadan *mandira*. Please continue showering your mercy on all of us so that we can serve you, your very dear Deities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārī, and all the Vaiṣṇavas until we leave our bodies. We have the tendency to run away from you; please hold our hands tightly so that we will always remain at your lotus feet.

Seeking the forgiveness and blessings of all your followers,  
Your humble and most fallen servants at Devasadan Mandira, Detroit, Michigan, USA.  
(written by Jagad Guru Dāsa)

## East Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*kṛṣṇa yadi kṛpā kare kona bhāgyavāne*  
*guru-antaryāmi-rūpe śikhāya āpane*

**Kṛṣṇa is situated in everyone’s heart as the** ***caittya-guru*****, the spiritual master within. When He is kind to some fortunate conditioned soul, He personally gives him lessons so he can progress in devotional service, instructing the person as the Supersoul within and the spiritual master without. \[*****Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā*** **22.47\]**

We have been hearing many of your disciples speak about their memories of you, glorifying you in so many magnificent ways. One thing that stands out in all of them is how much they love you. You have very expertly entered into the core of their hearts, like the Supersoul, and many years later you are still situated in their hearts, instructing them and reciprocating with them. You are always with them, and even though some may not follow everything perfectly, they cannot stop loving you. Anyone who met you and accepted you as their friend, father, well-wisher, or guru could not help but develop love for you. They could see how much you loved them, and naturally they reciprocated that love. You didn’t use magic or trickery, just pure love. In return they loved you, were willing to do anything for you, and continue loving you today, many years later. You melted their hearts, Śrīla Prabhupāda, with your love for Kṛṣṇa and all His parts and parcels. You are still in their hearts and are directing them back to Kṛṣṇa.

Because you love Kṛṣṇa, you have extended yourself and accepted us, and because Kṛṣṇa loves you, and is so pleased with you, one day we may also be allowed entrance into His transcendental domain. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your indebted servants at ISKCON East Hartford, Connecticut, USA.

## Eger

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

I would like to share with you a wonderful experience I had once while distributing books in downtown Debrecen, Hungary. I stopped a young lady and told her what I was doing. She said she knew the Hare Kṛṣṇas and had many of your books. I became curious and asked her which books she had. I learned that she had almost the whole *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* set and had read the first four cantos. I was amazed at how interested she was in reading your books, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Although people usually like to take your books from us, it is very rare when they really get absorbed in reading them. The conversation went on, and it came out that this young lady is chanting a few rounds of *japa* daily. I asked her why she took up chanting, and she replied, ”Because it is written in these books that chanting is very important.” So this young lady took up chanting before she had any association with other devotees; her only association was with you. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are such a wonderful personality! This young lady had never met you personally, but through your books she achieved your association, and you changed her life. She is on her way to Kṛṣṇa, back to the spiritual world.

It is impossible for me to estimate the spiritual potency of your books, the purity of your purports, or the devotion radiating from your books. You so much emphasized book distribution, and from this story we can all see that your books are like philosophers’ stones, which turn iron into gold. This encounter with the young lady was very encouraging for me: I could directly see the result of distributing your books.

*sādhūnāṁ sama-cittānāṁ sutarāṁ mat-kṛtātmanām*  
*darśanān no bhaved bandhaḥ puṁso ’kṣṇoḥ savitur yathā*

**When one is face to face with the sun, there is no longer darkness for one’s eyes. Similarly, when one is face to face with a** ***sādhu*****, a devotee, who is fully determined and surrendered to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, one will no longer be subject to material bondage. \[*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **10.10.41\]**

Your eternal servants at ISKCON Eger, Hungary.  
(written by Govinda Hari Dāsa)

## Finland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Last February we guided one group from the University of Helsinki on a tour of Vṛndāvana. After visiting our Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple and ISKCON’s center at Govardhana Hill, we took *prasādam* at the MVT. The teachers were so impressed that one Ph.D. said, “It seems you can get everything from your movement, both spiritual and material. You don’t really have to go outside to seek anything else.”

We certainly feel that you are seeing to our spiritual well-being. You are sending so many great souls here to enliven us during the year. Eight spiritual masters visit here regularly.

Kṛṣṇa is also sending us many students and other visitors all the time. Our devotees give many public lectures and even teach Kṛṣṇa consciousness officially in the schools. All this preaching enhances our understanding and appreciation of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

The philosophy coming through your books is the topmost. Kṛṣṇa consciousness also includes the highest and most pleasurable social life; even conditioned souls must admit that it is far beyond the social life available in material society. Still, in that field we have to make progress to reach the ultimate Vedic standard you describe in your books. That means *varṇāśrama-dharma*.

Even in the material sense there is so much support — the Bhaktivedanta Hospital, special Āyurvedic doctors, etc.

It is obvious that all the above-mentioned aspects of your mercy are just the tip of the iceberg. The list is actually endless. How fortunate we are that we have come to Kṛṣṇa consciousness at a time when so much still needs to be done to spread Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement!

Although we know it is never possible, our goal is to try to pay you back for all your mercy. Thus we are trying to distribute the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa according to the instructions in your books. The clear signs are everywhere: this world will change in the ways you have described in your books. It is only time that separates us from that transformation.

We pray at your lotus feet that we may stay in this wonderful shelter, ISKCON, for the rest of our lives. Though we recognize the imperfections of your Society, we follow a motto made popular during the British Empire: “ISKCON, with all thy faults, we still love thee.”

Your servants at the Finnish yātrā.  
(written by Tattvavāda Dāsa)

## Fortaleza

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances to your holy lotus feet. All glories to you!

We are immensely glad to write this offering today, in which we try to highlight a spark of Your Divine Grace’s opulence.

It was by the mercy of your direct disciples that the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement put down roots here in Fortaleza, but nowadays our congregation is exclusively composed of devotees of the second generation. Although no one here met you personally, it is impressive how much your instructions have transformed our lives, though sometimes we are too stubborn to follow you.

We are eternally grateful for all the efforts you made to bring Kṛṣṇa to our lives. Thank you so much for training and inspiring your powerful instruments, your sincere disciples, who taught us to walk firmly, although sometimes we need “crutches.”

Please keep blessing us in our humble attempts to spread your mission, so that this project becomes stronger and stronger for the glory of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We miss you a lot and wish to always remain your servants.  
Your servants at ISKCON Fortaleza, Brazil.

## Gainesville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances millions of times at your lotus feet. You are empowered to execute Lord Caitanya’s mission in this world. All glories to you!

I am writing from the site of the longest-running *prasādam* distribution program in ISKCON: the Kṛṣṇa Lunch at the University of Florida in Gainesville. Kṛṣṇa Lunch has been running for thirty-five years, but in the past two years distribution has doubled from an average of five hundred to nearly a thousand plates a day.

In Gainesville, *prasādam* has gone mainstream. Some students say their parents encourage them to eat Kṛṣṇa Lunch, just as they did. The university’s orientation book lists Kṛṣṇa Lunch as a recommended activity for incoming freshmen.

On June 29, 1971, you advised:

So do something wonderful there in Gainesville. Wonderful means simply you chant loudly and distribute *prasādam*. That is not very difficult. It is very easy. Simply if you do it enthusiastically and sincerely, then success will be there.

By your grace it is easy to see that the success of this program is due solely to following your glorious instructions. Thus I glorify you on your auspicious appearance day by praising your instructions.

Your instructions are glorious because:

- They are based on infallible *śāstra.*
- They spring from your natural Vaiṣṇava compassion, and thus are meant to lead people to the highest spiritual perfection—eternal love of God.
- You understand the transforming power of Kṛṣṇa’s holy name and *prasādam.*

Through faith in your instructions, your followers achieve faith in the holy name, *prasādam,* and all Vedic conclusions. Your instructions testify to your faith in Lord Caitanya’s prediction that His name will be heard in every town and village, and to your desire to make it happen. This project is flourishing through the power of your divine instructions. Seeing this, I can faithfully encourage those I meet to take your instructions seriously and flourish in every way.

Here devotees of all ages, from teenagers to seniors, work very cooperatively to carry out your wish for *prasādam* distribution and *kīrtana.* It reminds me of how you said, as you wound up your earthly pastimes, that we could please you by cooperating to execute your mission. I can see evidence of your pleasure in the steady expansion of this project, which is benefiting more and more people.

Thank you for your perfect instructions that result in the spiritual benefit of all. Thank you for bringing the radiant spiritual benediction of Lord Caitanya’s movement into this dark and dreary land. Please always engage us in executing your divine instructions.

Your faithful servants at ISKCON Gainesville, Florida, USA.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-kṛpā Dāsa)

## Gaṅgā-sāgara Baru

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi! All glories to Śrī Śrī RādhāŚyāmasundara! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Please allow me to present my Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. As a humble service, I have been entrusted to take up the *varṇāśrama-dharma* project here in Lupak, Central Borneo.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have inspired a project like Gaṅgā-sāgara Baru as a medium for performing devotional service Lord Kṛṣṇa. It allows me and others to more easily remember Lord Kṛṣṇa. By such mercy, we are not disturbed by the many complexities and problems most people face in order to acquire the basic necessities of life.

Here at Gaṅgā-sāgara Baru we focus on our devotional service by doing everything we can to follow your instructions about chanting the holy names in every town and village. We know that living on a farm is an important feature of Vaiṣṇava life, based as it is on simple living and pure thinking. We are thus spared from living in a crowded city, where life is filled with so many problems. We are always praying to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because without your kind blessings we cannot do anything.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are always praying to you and Lord Kṛṣṇa that sometime in the future Gaṅgā-sāgara Baru will be a village where many people who want to develop their devotional service can come and live. By your mercy, we believe that if we seriously perform our duties in devotional service, Gaṅgā-sāgara Baru will develop into the community you want it to be. As promised by Kṛṣṇa, wherever He is worshiped there must be happiness, peace, and prosperity. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this opportunity to serve you.

The devotees serving at Gaṅgā-sāgara Baru, ISKCON Kalimantan, Indonesia.  
(written by Vraja-rāja Dāsa)

## Gaura Hari Ashram (Indonesia)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu-nityānanda*  
*śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On 12 October, 1988, at 6:00 pm, I received initiation from His Holiness Kavicandra Swami. He is one of your best disciples who distributes your books, and by your mercy I have received mercy from the lotus feet of my spiritual master.

Since that time, day by day, my daily habits have changed. Before initiation my activities were for sense gratification, but now, by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* and following the four regulative principles, I am able to follow the path of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*. In this way I am engaged both morning and evening in *sādhana-bhakti*. I am also reading your books — *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* and *The Nectar of Devotion*. The more I read, and the more I become absorbed in chanting the holy names, the more my heart is satisfied. With those activities I pass my days in great happiness.

My material body is only a tool for me, the soul, to execute duties in service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, under the guidance of my spiritual master.

That Śrī Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead and all others are His subordinates becomes clearer to me day by day in my heart. This realization makes me become more diligent in discharging devotional service to Guru and Kṛṣṇa, following in your footsteps. There is nothing else in my life but performing devotional service to Guru and Kṛṣṇa.

*śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma, kevala bhakati-sadma,*  
*bando mui sāvadhāna mate*  
*jāhāra prasāde bhāi, e bhava toriyā jāi,*  
*kṛṣṇa-prāpti hoy jāhā hāte*

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava Mandir, Gaura Hari Ashram, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Maheśvāsa Dāsa)

## Gītā-nāgarī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

Although I am not in any way qualified to write this offering, I still would like to thank you with all my heart for blessing us all by giving us Gītā-nagarī farm. Even though some parts of the farm aren’t fully functional, the whole *dhāma* is magical nonetheless. You walked the dirt roads with your lotus feet and cast your spell. When I am quiet at night and gaze into the dark, silent woods, they seem to whisper “Hare Kṛṣṇa.” The sound of Kṛṣṇa’s cows munching on the lush green grass can be loud enough to wake me up at night. After I wake up I realize who is outside — just the cows eating. When they pause, it sounds like they are chanting softly. As I walk through the shadows on the creek road at dusk, past the water slowly bubbling by the cabin, all the way up to the old ox-power unit, I hear the birds and animals singing the holy name.

At Gītā-nagarī we all have our special places and our special sounds that we hear. By your mercy we can keep chanting, listening, and doing service here at Gītā- nāgarī farm.

The residents of Gītā-nāgarī, Pennsylvania, USA.  
(written by Jhulana-yātrā Devī Dāsī)

## Gītā-nāgarī Baru

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*mukhaṁ karoti vācālaṁ panguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu-nityānanda*  
*śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O Śrīla Gurudeva,

Your presence on this planet was evidence of Kṛṣṇa’s great mercy on the conditioned souls. You so expertly preached in the spirit of Lord Caitanya, distributing in every town and village the message of attaining love of God through chanting the holy name. You did not care who was qualified or unqualified: you gave us all so much transcendental knowledge.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O Patita-pāvana, you built a spiritual house in which everyone can understand the real goal of life. Success in devotional service is possible if we associate with sādhus, pure devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa, always chant the holy name, regularly hear the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, live in a holy place, honor *prasādam,* etc. — all activities you describe in your books.

In this age the social situation is very, very dangerous (*padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadām*), but because of your mercy, everything is being changed. Now we are trying to fulfill your desire to establish *varṇāśrama-dharma,* and our first step is to build this Vaiṣṇava village called Gītā-nāgarī Baru. Because you poured your blessings upon them, your disciples His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami and His Grace Gaura-maṇḍala Bhūmi Prabhu are always giving us excellent guidance for advancing in devotional service and establishing *varṇāśrama*. We live in the Kṛṣṇa conscious atmosphere of this village without electricity or other modern amenities. We work as farmers and teachers. We try to follow your motto of “simple living and high thinking.” We grow rice, vegetables, cassava, dāl, fruit, etc., and we eat only what we grow. We are ninety-four devotees, comprising twenty-three *gṛhastha* families. Our buildings include the Rādhā-Madana-Gopāla temple, the office, the Bhaktivedanta Library, and the Gopāla children’s school. Our land covers sixty square kilometers. Besides farming, our activities include putting on festivals and conducting seminars. Many guests come to see the Deities of the Lord, read your books in the library, etc.

All this is possible only because of your blessings. We thank you for your mercy so we can make our lives successful. We try to always remember Lord Kṛṣṇa, twenty-four hours a day, as you taught by your example. Please bless us so we can be liberated from *māyā* and engage in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa and our spiritual master:
