*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ‘pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥśrī-caraṇāravindam*

In addition, we are hoping to receive your blessings so that we can help you spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement of chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*,

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

Thank you again for everything, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants at Gītā-nāgarī Baru, Lampung, Indonesia.  
(written by Ṣaḍbhuja Gaurāṅga Dāsa)

## Gothenburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

The past year has been a year of change and renewal in your temple in \[fix\]Gothenburg. Some devotees have left for other places of service, and some devotees have come here to try to serve Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and yourself. We have been trying to develop deeper relationships among the devotees and focus on quality association and preaching to the devotees.

All of us have experienced your presence within the *vaiṣṇava-saṅga.* This *saṅga* has been the driving factor in our efforts to please you and your servants.

We all have found great inspiration in serving you and pray for your blessings to increase our efforts and find the strength and purity to attract more people to your glorious and wonderful movement.

Please give us the inspiration to find novel ways to reach people’s hearts and minds in attractive ways and to give one another all the love and trust we need to continue in our service.

We are eternally grateful for your kindness and service to the world. By the merciful glance of a pure Vaiṣṇava like yourself, it is possible for conditioned souls like us to make advancement and be accepted into Kṛṣṇa’s loving family.

Please continue to glance over us and forgive our short-comings and childish mistakes in serving you.

Your grateful servants at ISKCON Gothenburg, Sweden.

## Honduras

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

I think that for a spiritual institution to succeed in its mission, which is to take the conditioned souls back to Godhead, to Kṛṣṇa, it has to strictly follow the founder-*ācārya’s* mood.

What was the mood of our Śrīla Prabhupāda? Give Kṛṣṇa consciousness, give Kṛṣṇa consciousness, give Kṛṣṇa consciousness without wanting anything in return. That is the mood of Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotees. The appearance of a personality like Śrīla Prabhupāda is destined to be a landmark in history. Now we say that we are in year 2007 of the Christian era, but one day people will say, “We are in year 4,327 of the Prabhupāda era.” As he said, his books will be the law books for humanity for the next 10,000 years.

We can meditate on how to make Śrīla Prabhupāda happy; we all know that he is very pleased to see that his books are distributed. There is no doubt that when we go out on the street on *saṅkīrtana,* we develop humility and tolerance and are forced to give up our false ego.

I beg Śrīla Prabhupāda that wherever I may be, let me have some of his books in my hand, either for studying or for distributing. I have full faith that this is the safest position in the three worlds.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants at ISKCON Honduras.  
(written by Haripāda Dāsa)

## Johannesburg (Lenasia)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Loving Grace! All glories to your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā!

In 1975 you mercifully visited our city.  
Despite the country’s politics, you preached vigorously.  
You were “an Indian Swami” with a difference:  
No compromise! Saintly in appearance.  
Now our congregation consists of mostly granddisciples  
Drawing inspiration from your various books and articles.  
During the year many seminars based on your teachings are held,  
Whereby, via your teachings, true religion is upheld.  
Feeding the needy here, as per your desire, earns media attention.  
This activity is inspired by your unyielding compassion.  
It is yet another avenue you afforded us for our purification.  
We pray to continue this humble service to your mission.  
An increase in regular studies of your writings we attempted.  
Thus many through Bhaktivedanta College have graduated.  
Bhakti-śāstrī to Bhakti-vaibhava is in session.  
Classes are held weekly for such spiritual absorption.  
Festivals you taught us to celebrate attract new souls,  
Who bring with them the ecstasy of increased service goals.  
These opportunities leave us eager with ideas to improve.  
Thus follow-up meetings help us in our forward move.  
The Deity worship you gave to us we regard as sacred.  
We try to maintain strict standards and the Deities are admired.  
For this personal worship you gave to us we remain indebted.  
So many opportunities for purification you have to us allotted.  
On this auspicious day we pray to become worthy servants,  
To be able to render future service with notable improvements,  
To serve your mission pure-heartedly,  
And to chant the *mahā-mantra* regularly and attentively.  
You are our pre-eminent *śikṣā-guru,* our most worthy master.  
We remain ever dependent on your shelter.  
We pray to serve you with strong faith.  
With folded hands, we your blessings forever await.

Your servants at ISKCON Lenasia, Johannesburg, South Africa.  
(written by Mādrī Devī Dāsī)

## Karuṇā Bhavān

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to you!

Thank you for coming to this godforsaken place, and thank you for staying. You have given the whole world an exit from the ruthless and cruel material world — an opportunity to go home, back to Godhead.

Kali-yuga is an ocean of faults, and also an ocean of fault-finding, but by Kṛṣṇa’s grace and your unsurpassed example you are faultless. No one else could ever achieve what you did by distributing Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world. Therefore you are the most worshipable personality in all the three worlds.

By serving your lotus feet and by your pleasure, we know that Lord Kṛṣṇa is pleased. By serving those great Vaiṣṇavas who are pleasing you, we know that the whole *paramparā* is pleased. By serving the servants of the servants of the servants of Kṛṣṇa, a million times removed, we become all the more in line for the Lord’s mercy. Therefore you are the spiritual master of the whole world.

Before you embarked on your mission to the West in 1965, the vast majority of the world’s population had never heard of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Caitanya Mahāprabhu, Vṛndāvana-dhāma, etc. Maybe there were some speculative treatises by offensive and ignorant impersonalists or envious demons. But you gave the world Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava, Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma, the Lord’s associates, pastimes, and devotional service — the whole spiritual world. You didn’t give some fancy imaginative mental concoction, but you gave knowledge revealed by the confidential understanding of the Vedic literature. When the sun rises at the beginning of a new day, it does not create the hills, rivers, forests, cities, hermitages, animals, society, etc; rather, by its light one can see everything as it is. Similarly, the *ācārya* reveals the truth that already exists; he does not create anything artificial. And so you have revealed the actual nature of Godhead in all His aspects. Therefore you are the sun that diminishes the glory of the puffed-up and arrogant atheistic glowworms who seem to flourish without restraint in the darkness of Kali-yuga. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your books are stupendous: transcendental literature that is creating a revolution in the impious lives of the world’s misdirected civilization. You not only behaved as the *mahā-bhāgavata* devotee of the Lord that you were, preaching *bhāgavata-dharma,* but you simultaneously and tirelessly worked to establish in practice, through your ISKCON, the knowledge in your books. You wanted your disciples (and everyone else) to read and study your books. You said that even by touching one of your books or reading even one word a person would be immensely benefited. To assimilate this knowledge, put it into practice under the guidance of the previous *ācāryas,* and then distribute it — this is the perfect engagement. “If you really want to please me, then distribute my books.”

Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself said to the cowherd boys: “It is the duty of every living being to perform welfare activities for the benefit of others with his life, wealth, intelligence, and words.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 10.22.35)

Simply by our taking part in this *saṅkīrtana* mission of Lord Śrī Caitanya according to our capacity and without envy, everyone is perfectly engaged. Even though by our low and degraded birth and our conditioned nature we are apparently disqualified, still you have given us (and everyone else) the opportunity to be engaged in the Lord’s service and perfect our lives. You are therefore certainly the most exalted and wonderful shelter for the whole world.

We pray that we may always be engaged in your service in the association of the host of wonderful devotees who serve your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! And all glories to your transcendental limbs, the great souls who are helping you execute your mission of distributing the soothing rays of the supremely merciful sun and moon that have arisen on the horizon of Gauḍadeśa.

vande ’haṁśrī-guroḥśrī-yuta-pada-kamalaṁśrī-gurūn vaiṣṇavāṁś ca

I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master, of all other preceptors on the path of devotional service, and of all Vaiṣṇavas.

*yadā parānanda-guro bhavat-pade*  
*padaṁ mano me bhagaval labheta*  
*tadā nirastākhila-sādhana-śramaḥ*  
*śrayeya saukhyaṁ bhavataḥ kṛpātaḥ*

**O transcendentally blissful guru, when my mind finally achieves a place at your lotus feet, all the tiresome labor of my spiritual practices will be finished, and by your mercy I will experience the greatest happiness.**

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Your servants at Karuṇā Bhavān, Scotland.

## Kecskemet

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to you!

Every year when we write an homage to you, our heart sinks, since we would like to satisfy you with our whole year’s activities, development, and results. We hear from your disciples or read in your teachings that if we are sincere in our efforts and want to serve you without selfish interests, results will surely come.

We know you liked great efforts, “trying to shoot the rhinoceros,” and spectacular successes and development. Every year we make plans and would like to exceed our expectations, thus assuring that we receive your blessings.

Often, however, our results are not so spectacular. Yet we take hope from your example of steadfastness and commitment in the face of adversity while attempting to fulfill the wishes of your spiritual master. From the very beginning of your preaching mission in the West, when you were all alone and results were yet to come, you showed that a willingness to undergo austerities and sacrifice everything with unflinching determination and enthusiasm is necessary to achieve success.

Our center is small, but the devotees work with great effort to acquaint people with Kṛṣṇa consciousness in such a way that they will esteem it. We continually distribute your books, attract supporters, and present the movement in a positive way so that the leaders of our town will favorably acknowledge our mission.

We beg you to bless us with the spiritual strength and intelligence to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness successfully here. This is our very life.

Thank you again for your tireless and selfless preaching efforts, by which you enabled us to practice genuine spiritual life.

Your servants in Kecskemet, Hungary.  
(written by Dharmiṣṭhā Devī Dāsī)

## KwaZulu Natal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

By your mercy we have again completed a successful Ratha-yātrā, or Festival of the Chariots. Thousands of conditioned souls were exposed to the blissful life of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the life you brought to the world with great sacrifice and compassion.

Throughout this Ratha-yātrā festival, we were reminded of that sacrifice and compassion. Through that reminder we realize that there is so much for us yet to do in line with your desires for South Africa.

I am especially happy to hear that you are making some local African boys and girls into devotees, that I wanted. Preaching is our real business, not getting big, big buildings and doing business for money just to maintain them, no. \[Letter to Cyavana11 November 1972\]

We have not made significant progress with regards to African preaching — our real business. So while there may be mammoth festivals and other Kṛṣṇa conscious activities, we feel somewhat at a loss writing this year’s Vyāsa-pūjā offering.

We also want to please you and make you “especially happy” with our efforts in South Africa. Slowly we are seeing that the focus for ISKCON in South Africa is gravitating toward African preaching — in the townships, universities, etc.

We know that this will especially please you, and we are dependent on your mercy to be successful, even slightly.

As evidence of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mercy, we are experiencing that the people of Africa are taking to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, chanting and dancing and taking *prasādam* like other Vaiṣṇavas. This is all due to the power of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Who can understand how His potency is working all over the world? \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 17.54, purport)\]

Who can understand how His potency is working all over the world? We dare to say that we can. Lord Caitanya’s potency, fully invested in your efforts, your arrangements, and your desires is working all over the world.

*kali-kālera dharma—kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana*

**“The fundamental religious system in the Age of Kali is the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Unless empowered by Kṛṣṇa, one cannot propagate the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **movement.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Antya*** **7.11)**

We pray to be firm in resolution, fixed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and pure in motive. In this way we hope to attract your mercy. How else can we be imbued with the required potency of Lord Caitanya to fulfill our “real business” in South Africa.

Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants at ISKCON KwaZulu-Natal, South Africa.

## Lisbon

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dearest master, please accept our deepest gratitude for the love you show to all the living entities, especially to us fallen followers of yours. You are kindly teaching and disciplining us so that one day we may become true devotees of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Many of us are enthused by your perfect example and deep love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and despite great difficulties, devotees remain steadfast in their prescribed duties only due to the example of your immaculate divine love.

You unconditionally dedicate your attention to those who show the slightest interest in serving the Lord, just as a loving mother unreservedly loves her children, and you are worshiped by disciples and followers in the same mood.

Although we are unqualified, you show us what is truly valuable and make us believe that we can eventually overcome our limitations and serve Kṛṣṇa appropriately. You taught us about what was completely unknown to us — the Paramātmā, the omnipresent Lord who is with us at every moment of our lives in our hearts and is the source of everything within and without us.

Your Divine Grace has taught us to appreciate in others any brightness they may manifest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. This was your example, the example of a true servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

We pray onto your lotus feet so that we may continue in your service.

Your humble servants in the Lisbon yātrā, Portugal.

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya*

**Make the teachings emanating from the lotus mouth of our spiritual master one with your heart. \[Narottama Dāsa Ṭhākura\]**

The very first instruction given to you by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura was to take the message of Lord Caitanya to the English-speaking people. He himself kept a map of London on the wall of his room at the Caitanya Maṭha and spoke of the day when a *harināma saṅkīrtana* party would sing the holy names in the streets around Buckingham Palace. He envisioned a place where the young people of England could come to live and learn about Kṛṣṇa. You made your spiritual master’s instructions one with your heart and, in doing so, made his vision for London a reality within a short time.

*Kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana.* One can only preach successfully when empowered by Kṛṣṇa; and only one who has dedicated his life to his *guru* will be empowered by Kṛṣṇa. Through your determination to carry out the order of your spiritual master, no matter if you faced hardship or apparent failure, a beautiful temple appeared in the heart of London. How could this have happened without your devotion? Only if Kṛṣṇa makes an arrangement for His pure devotee can a small group of penniless youngsters open a branch of an unknown spiritual movement in the heart of a decidedly materialistic city.

As if to reveal that He was indeed present in London and always guiding you, the Lord then visibly manifested in the most beautiful forms of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Almost effortlessly They came into your hands and, upon your invitation, agreed to accept worship from your young followers. In keeping with ancient Vaiṣṇava tradition, you incorporated the name of the city into the names of the Deities, indicating that although the Lord is ever-present in the eternal realm of Goloka, even more important for us, His devotees, is that He is now present in the city of London.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught your earliest followers in London how to artistically sing and dance for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, and you were happy when they informed you that, according to the newspapers, the Kṛṣṇa chant had startled London. You brought the gorgeous Ratha-yātrā Festival and danced with your devotees in a joyful and unprecedented manner through the streets of London. Your spiritual master must have beamed with happiness on that day.

You said that four of your unique contributions to the West — those experiences that no other spiritual teacher had given to the public before — were Deity worship, large-scale festivals, mass distribution of sanctified food, and wide dissemination of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and other Vedic scriptures. You asked your disciples and followers to continue to develop all these activities right here in London.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is now 2007 and all of us here at the Soho Street temple feel so privileged to be playing a part in your historic mission to bring Kṛṣṇa consciousness to Britain. Sometimes we find it hard to imagine why such a sacred task has now been left to us. We echo your instructions when we declare it to everyone that our mission is to flood London with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And yet we feel that we are hardly qualified for this task. Some days we feel that we must have simply arrived here at Soho Street by some accident or a coincidental chain of events. Certainly, we can never imagine that we have been hand-picked by you for this great task.

And yet we know from your teachings that nothing and no one moves in this world without the will of the Supreme Lord, and that therefore, if we are members of the community of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, it is only by your arrangement for Them.

Feeling unqualified yet specially blessed by you makes us even more determined to be worthy of your trust in us. You have placed us here, and now we must rise to become those Vaiṣṇavas you need to complete your mission for London.

So we begin each day by gathering before you and praising you as the confidential servant of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara. We acknowledge our deepest debt to you, and thank you for being the channel of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy that rains upon us every new morning.

On this holy day, the anniversary of your appearance in this world, we, the devotees who join together in this temple, our spiritual oasis in the heart of London, ask for your continued blessings and prayers to Kṛṣṇa for our welfare and the success of your mission. Specifically, we request you to pray for our growth and strength in the tasks you have set for us:

May we have the strength to always rise early and follow the life-giving morning *sādhana* you gave us. May we chant the holy names on our beads, here or in our homes, and study together the brilliant *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. We know that this is our spiritual foundation and the support for everything else we do.

May we have the love to always decorate and offer service to your beloved Rādhā-Londonīśvara. By offering them delicious eatables, performing the *ārati* ceremony (to which even Lord Śiva and Lord Brahmā come), and singing and dancing before Them, we know that our hard hearts will become softened and our love for the things of this world will diminish to nothing.

May we have the kindness of spirit to always distribute *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*, one of the powerful medicines for the disease of material existence. May the rich and the poor, the simple and the sophisticated, all have the chance to experience the sanctification available from eating the Lord’s remnants.

May we have courage and dedication to share Kṛṣṇa’s names so that the streets of London always echo to the sound of the *mahā-mantra*, the beating of the *mṛdaṅgas*, and the chiming of the *karatālas*.

May we always please you by distributing your books, may we encourage and guide others to distribute your books, and may the words of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* find a place on the bookshelves, in the homes, and on the tongues of the millions of souls in London.

And finally, Śrīla Prabhupāda, may we have the blessing that we can help you satisfy your Guru Mahārāja by, as he requested, setting up a student hostel in London where many young people can learn to be free through your gracious teachings, and that, by their joining our number, the movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu will become more widely established in what you sometimes called “the most important city in the world.”

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at ISKCON London (Soho), UK.

## London (south)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you and your wonderful ISKCON movement.

By your mercy this year has been one of growth for South London. Under the guidance of your disciple His Holiness Kṛṣṇa Dāsa Swami Mahārāja, we have managed to re-create a wonderful Sunday feast program as you desired for all your centers around the world. Simply by our implementing your magical formula of singing, dancing, and feasting, the wonderful Sunday program has become famous in the local area and people are coming to take refuge from the harshness of material life and engage in the worship of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you set the example of how to preach through regular street *harināma*, and by this people are able to hear and are being attracted to the wonderful holy names of the Lord. By your mercy we are helping to fulfill the prophecy of Lord Caitanya that His name will be spread to every town and village, as given by your practical example.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, had it not been for you, I cannot imagine the deep dark well of ignorance we would have been lost in, and this shining oasis in the midst of South London would have been just another building.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you so much for your kind blessings and all that you have done for us. However, I know if we really appreciated your gifts we would increase our *harināma*, increase our book distribution, and increase our attendance at *maṅgala-ārati* in order to progress nicely on this path of devotional service and preaching. We beg for your mercy so that we can nicely undertake these activities and create a wonderful center for you.

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON London (south), UK.  
(written by Sāndīpani Muni Dāsa)

## Malaga

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

To glorify you on this auspicious day of your appearance is a wonderful opportunity and at the same time a great challenge. How can we, who are all prisoners of this material world, bound by the three modes of material nature, say something meaningful about a personality beyond material existence, a resident of the spiritual realm? Only by your causeless mercy are we capable of appreciating to some small degree your sublime qualities and the unlimited benefit your appearance is showering on our lives. Almost none of us here at the Malaga temple had the good fortune of meeting you personally, but we are relishing your association by reading your books, listening to your lectures, worshiping your *mūrti* in the temple, and serving your dedicated followers.

Every week, as part of the morning program, we listen to one of your classes on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and then exchange our realizations about the philosophical points you make. We experience how the transcendental sound vibration emanating from your lotus lips destroys ignorance and extinguishes the blazing fire of material existence. Your words are evidence of your vast erudition, your penetrating intelligence, and your deep love and devotion for Kṛṣṇa. Your love for Kṛṣṇa and your compassion for the fallen souls are so powerful that they transform anyone who comes in contact with you. We are sure that is only a matter of time before the whole world will appreciate your divine qualities and will adopt the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Although we are still struggling to become fixed in devotional service, we are hopeful that by our sincere effort to assist you in your mission to propagate Kṛṣṇa consciousness you will be pleased with us and shower your mercy upon us. In this way we may become qualified to create an auspicious atmosphere and transform this temple into a spiritual oasis, a place of refuge for all those who are eager to go back home, back to Godhead.

Your servants at ISKCON Malaga, Spain.  
(written by Rasāmṛta Devī Dāsī)

## Manchester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is our unlimitedly great fortune to have your association in the form of your devotees, your books, and your glorious temples.

Most of us here in Manchester never met you personally. However, all of us are touched by your compassion and feel safe and secure at your lotus feet. Your glories are endless. We find ourselves incapable of making any attempt to glorify you with our words. However, we will make an attempt anyway, hoping that you will be pleased.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is said that all perfection is bestowed upon a disciple if he simply serves his spiritual master with humility. You revealed to us that ISKCON is your body. It is our great fortune to serve you personally by serving your temples, your books, and your devotees.

And what is your temple?

It is proved time and time again by every one who visits your temple that it is the place where suffering conditioned souls feel sheltered.

Your temple is a place where they find hope and feel joyful after being tormented by the material energy again and again, day after day, birth after birth.

Your temple is a place where they are given an opportunity to participate in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Gaurāṅga.

Your temple is a place where everyone has an opportunity to utilize his or her precious human birth in serving Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and Their beloved devotees.

Your temple is a place where one can associate with a genuine sādhu at a time when the word *sādhu* has literally lost its meaning in this world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, when we are engaged in serving your temples we can easily realize that you truly built a house where the whole world can live.

Because it is Kali-yuga, we understand that difficulties and problems are expected in every service we do. However, we know that these difficulties are the lingering effects of our own attachments and previous sinful activities, returning to trouble us just for our own purification. Thus when we are purified by trying to overcome these difficulties, and when we fully utilize the transcendental intelligence you are giving us to remember that we are all the eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa, we will be better able to serve your temples, your books, and your devotees. Difficulties and obstacles are simply an opportunity to surrender to your lotus feet.

And what are your books?

They are the life-blood of the devotees who desire to understand you and your mission.

They are the very means by which we fallen souls can hear pure *kṛṣṇa-kathā* and see the self-effulgent standard of purity of the Absolute Truth.

They are the glasses by which our defective vision is corrected.

They are the brilliant sunlight to expose all the dark misconceptions and speculations of material existence and dispel the darkest ignorance accumulated over so many births.

They are the expert gardening guide by which the weeds of the threefold miseries of worldly life that doggedly follow our every materially inclined thought, word, and deed are uprooted.

They are the timeless messages of the Lord transmitted by the pure devotee *ācāryas*, enabling anyone fortunate enough to contact them to be instantly plugged in and thus become, knowingly or unknowingly, electrified by those messages.

They are the open doorway from the labyrinthine material existence, showing the straightforward shining and safe path back to the service, abode, and lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

They are the sweet taste of the ocean of *prema-bhakti*, distributed freely by Lord Gaurāṅga and His servants, to overflood the population of the entire material creation that is struggling with the endless desert of dull wrangling in material existence.

They are the life-giving rescue rope of hope, shelter, protection, and factual understanding for those who are constantly being attacked by the sharks, alligators, and *timiṅgilas* of atheistic, impersonalistic, and Māyāvāda philosophies that pervade and flourish throughout the dangerous ocean of Kali and that destroy all the good qualities of the human beings.

They are the very means by which the devotees can fulfill the meaning of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s statement in the *Bhagavad-gītā* 10.9:

The thoughts of My pure devotees dwell in Me, their lives are fully devoted to My service, and they derive great satisfaction and bliss from always enlightening one another and conversing about Me.

They are the means by which we can assist you in your mission, given to you by your beloved Guru Mahārāja, to take everyone back home, back to Godhead. We can do so by hearing and explaining them, by sharing them with others, and by, in our insignificant and paltry way, facilitating their distribution as widely as possible to this world’s misdirected civilization.

They are the means by which we can always remember you as our master, feel grateful and obliged to you for your kind gift of transcendental knowledge, and purify ourselves in your association as you bask in the ecstasies of loving Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

And who are your devotees?

From what we have understood, they are those who in some way or other, from the most lowly to the most advanced, take pleasure in serving you and your mission, which is to give us all back our life and bring us back to the shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

They are your beloved servants and children, whom we must learn to serve, associate with appropriately, and co-operate with to please you and carry out your mission.

They are your descendants, gradually learning how and why to carefully grow and develop their spiritual lives in the nurturing association of your ISKCON, to carry on the family business, which you are entrusting to us all.

They are our prabhus, our masters, to help us and be served by us so that we may advance ever closer to the shade of your beautiful golden lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this very auspicious occasion of your appearance day celebration in the year 2007, we lay down our heads at your lotus feet and beg that you may be pleased with us and bestow upon us the power to help and serve everyone who wants to take shelter at your lotus feet.

Please be compassionate upon us and grant us the benediction that may we all remain united in serving your divine body of ISKCON. Please grant us the benediction that may we keep chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa no matter what situation we are in, both collectively and individually.

All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu! All glories to your ISKCON, your temples, your books, and your servants!

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Manchester, UK.

## Markham/Richmond Hill

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Prabhupāda is . . . our Beacon.  
We were aimlessly pursuing Māyās attraction,  
Not knowing that this was causing our spirituality to weaken,  
But you washed away our darkness by giving us ISKCON.  
Prabhupāda is . . . our Jewel.  
Before you rescued us we were acting like fools.  
You would challenge your detractors in philosophical duel;  
And you wanted to ensure proper training by establishing *gurukul*.

Prabhupāda is . . . Determined.  
In this material world we were bound and conditioned,  
Not knowing that what we were doing was causing us to be ruined,  
But you fought all obstacles and objections to ensure we were enlightened.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Author.  
We were weak and lacking spiritual power.  
Thus you instructed us with numerous letters,  
And in the period of only twelve years wrote so many books so that we would not falter.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Spiritual Guide.  
We were not happy no matter how hard we tried;  
By giving us Kṛṣṇa you saved us from Māyās stormy tide;  
You gave us the solution to escape from Kali-yuga’s conflict and divide.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Physician.  
We were pursuing our false pride and ambition,  
But you provided us the cure from this material condition.  
Now all we want to do is keep taking this spiritual prescription.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Mentor.  
We were living this life as if we were front and center,  
Not knowing that this is temporary and we are just renters.  
You dispelled this nonsense and became our trusted counselor.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Messenger.  
You came to the West on your spiritual master’s order,  
To impart the scriptures as Kṛṣṇa’s perfect narrator.  
You described Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes and spoke *kṛṣṇa-kathā* in every word you would utter.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Relief.  
We were waddling in a world of suffering and grief,  
Not knowing that what we were thinking was actually an erroneous belief,  
But you blessed us by becoming our commander in chief.

Prabhupāda is . . . the Authority,  
You defeated professors and scholars with divine philosophy,  
Smashing nonsense arguments with great ability.  
You were the final word, using spiritual knowledge and making Kṛṣṇa the priority.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Benefactor.  
In a web we were being held by Māyā, our captor,  
Thinking that of this life we were the proprietor,  
But you gave us Kṛṣṇa consciousness and became mankind’s rescuer.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Disciplinarian.  
We were behaving like spoiled children,  
But like a father you would lovingly correct us and give us the foundation,  
Your instructions and words are for us like a clarion.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Real Friend.  
You effectively showed us that what we were doing was just pretend,  
That on these material relationships we could not depend.  
You gave us devotional service so this material world we could transcend.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Exemplar.  
We had no one to follow, as many preach one thing and do another,  
But you led by example with your own flawless behavior,  
Giving us the utmost model of an impeccable leader.

Prabhupāda is . . . Bona Fide,  
You ensured your teachings were for everyone, no matter where they reside.  
You proved time and time again that by Kṛṣṇa’s laws we should abide.  
Never again will we come across someone as purified.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Salvation.  
You are delivering us from this life of misery through your kind association.  
By rescuing us with your mercy and explaining self-realization,  
You showed us that you are our savior taking us to Goloka Vṛndāvana.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Captain.  
For us to become soldiers for Kṛṣṇa is what you wanted to attain.  
To win this battle against Māyās forces, the fight we must maintain.  
Because you are our commander, we know that our mission will not be in vain.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Affection.  
We were surrounding ourselves with false connections,  
Not knowing that we were just engaging in sense gratification,  
But you lovingly took us under your wing and gave us protection.

Prabhupāda is . . . Compassionate.  
From this misery the fallen souls you wanted to alleviate.  
Everyone about Kṛṣṇa consciousness you wanted to educate.  
To give this wonderful opportunity to all was what you wanted to facilitate.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Liaison.  
Between us and Kṛṣṇa, you were the one to bridge the gap of separation.  
Because of you we were able to see your divine vision.  
To get back to Godhead, you are the sole reason.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Well-wisher.  
We thought that money and self-satisfaction were the answer,  
But to show us the right path you were the perfect instructor.  
Coming into our lives, you gave us the most precious favor.

Prabhupāda is . . . the Most Learned.  
We were working hard to get materialistically ahead.  
By your perfect teachings we knew that we should follow you instead.  
You guided us through Vedic knowledge in everything you said.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Saṅkīrtana Leader.  
Through the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa you engaged even the bystander.  
Establishing over one hundred temples, you were Lord Caitanya’s builder.  
You spread festivals and *harinām* across every continent and border.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Freedom.  
The opportunity to be free from repetition of birth is so seldom.  
You showed us that to achieve this is not something random,  
But can be done by surrendering to Kṛṣṇa and following your wisdom.

Prabhupāda is . . . Bold.  
You came to America at the advanced age of sixty-nine years old.  
Your Guru Mahārāja’s instructions you came to uphold.  
You would impart all that we should know in the morning as you strolled.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Aim.  
We were trapped in the clutches of darkness before you came.  
By your grace Hare Kṛṣṇa enjoys worldwide fame.  
We wish to forever glorify you and chant the holy name.

Prabhupāda is . . . Qualified.  
Instructions, temples, and Deities you did provide.  
You successfully established ISKCON’s presence far and wide.  
We hope that our love for you and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa will never subside.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Divine Grace.  
No matter what obstacles we may face,  
No matter the time or the place,  
It is your mission that we wish to forever embrace.

Prabhupāda is . . . our Guru.  
There is so much we owe you.  
By your mercy and grace we are some of the lucky few.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you, thank you, thank you.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya Gurudeva!

Your humble servants in Markham and Richmond Hill, Canada.  
(written by Mīnākṣī Devī Dāsī)

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you! You have lifted us out of the otherwise endless cycle of birth, disease, old age, and death, where we have been simply chewing the chewed since time immemorial. Through your books and other instructions you have sharpened our intelligence, given us the opportunity to see the world in proper perspective, and helped us understand the superiority of transferring ourselves to the eternal spiritual kingdom to engage fully in uninterrupted devotional service.

You boldly taught us that to become Kṛṣṇa conscious requires that we accept the Absolute Truth with firm faith and devotion, even if we do not fully understand it and it differs from the viewpoint of the materialistic scientists and politicians. As a pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, you had such faith and devotion in the Lord. Consequently, you could see everything with crystal clarity and were never in illusion. Whether the topic was spiritual realization or scientific achievements or political upheavals, you could see everything in relation to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

In the 1960s, even as the world supposedly watched man take his first steps on the moon, you, relying fully on the Vedic conclusion, emphatically stated that man never went to the moon and would never be able to do so. Many people then — even some of your disciples — did not accept the Vedic conclusion. Still you did not change your statement, because it was based on the Absolute Truth. With time, investigations have proven to millions that in fact man did not go to the moon and that the government staged a great hoax to cheat the people.

It is not insignificant, however, that people are inclined to search out the truth. In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* you explained that the principles of religion, namely cleanliness, truthfulness, austerity, and mercy, have been practically destroyed over time. Only truthfulness remains to some degree, and that is being quickly lost due to widespread deceit. Our inclination to understand the truth is based ultimately on the constitution of the soul, which is eternal, blissful, and full in knowledge. Knowledge means to understand the difference between matter and spirit and to understand that Lord Kṛṣṇa is the supreme controller. It is therefore human nature to search out the truth, and that inclination for many was expressed as an investigation into the Apollo Moon Mission. The evidence that was uncovered led millions of Americans to seriously doubt the propaganda from the government that we landed men on the moon. This incident served as a heavy blow against the dream of many that modern science will eventually solve all the problems of life. You predicted that in the near future people will lose all hope that material science can solve the miseries of life. We can see this actually happening.

Ultimately, the only truth that will satisfy the people is the truth about Lord Kṛṣṇa and our relationship with Him. You have given us that highest truth, and your mention of other events, like the moon landing and the cheating government leaders, only proves the validity of the conclusion of the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* that we must surrender to Lord Kṛṣṇa. A prisoner who is content with his life in prison has no impetus to reform his life and leave the prison. Similarly, unless we become fully convinced that material existence is miserable and there is no chance of solving our problems through material means like scientific advancement, we will never have the resolute determination to engage in devotional service and become free from our material entanglement.

You assured us that the people in general will become so disturbed by the suffering imposed by their governments and by the other threefold miseries that they will come to our temples and farms by the thousands to find relief. Here at ISKCON Miami we realize that we have a grave responsibility to the people in general. We are trying to organize our temple in such a way that we can become greatly instrumental in the establishment of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Coconut Grove and the world.

Our temple is called New Naimiṣāraṇya Forest because it was in Naimiṣāraṇya Forest at the beginning of Kali-yuga that the highest truth, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* was discussed. It is our desire and plan to insure that Vedic knowledge is forever and increasingly discussed and practiced at our temple, for only the discussion and propagation of such transcendental subject matter can fully satisfy the hearts of the living entities.

Although this task sometimes seems impossible, we have faith that if we do everything that we can to carry on the *saṅkīrtana* movement, Lord Kṛṣṇa will carry what we lack. This is the example that you set. You came to America with practically nothing but a few books, your deep faith in Lord Kṛṣṇa, and your desire to fulfill the mission given by your spiritual master. You dedicated your life fully to that mission, and Lord Kṛṣṇa carried what you lacked and made this movement successful. We know that if we can follow your example and instructions we will also have all success.

It is our desire and conviction that Kṛṣṇa consciousness will become very prominent in Coconut Grove and that thousands of people will reap the wonderful results of devotional service to the Lord. We will continue to encourage people to take up this way of life, become initiated and eventually second initiated, and fully engaged in devotional service. It is our goal to establish a *varṇāśrama* college that will teach everything needed to become Kṛṣṇa conscious and spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Spiritual education is a vital part of establishing *varṇāśrama-dharma*, a great task that you left for us to do. The *varṇāśrama* college will teach the Kṛṣṇa consciousness philosophy to the highest level. It will also teach all aspects of Deity worship, including cooking, sewing, jewelry making, and more. It will also teach many other courses necessary for preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness and managing this great movement.

Although we have only a few devotees living in the temple, we have an increasing number of active devotees in the congregation. We are confident that by your mercy everything will increase here beyond anyone’s expectations. We have established a group of devotees known as the SSPT, Spiritual Strategic Planning Team. We are seeing that the SSPT team is gradually increasing in size and involvement. We are confident that if we continue to chant sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra* daily and follow the four regulative principles, Lord Caitanya will bless us with wonderful results.

Thank you so much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all that you have given us. We always pray that we may come to a higher standard of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and increase our effectiveness in spreading the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Miami, Florida, USA.

## Mumbai (Mira Road)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you!To remember, glorify, and serve such a pure devotee as you is the perfection of one’s life. Therefore on this most auspicious day of your appearance, we all fall down in the dust of your divine lotus feet and pray:

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In our effort to remember and glorify you, we try to offer you not only flowers, *ārati,* and delicious preparations but also deeds according to your desire. We hear about you from your wonderful disciples, who explain to us that on the spiritual platform you are always present and that you are always protecting ISKCON by your great spiritual power. Your divine instructions are a torchlight for us.

There is no doubt that you are here with us today for this gathering. You are our only hope, and we realize that we are all made of your mercy,

Without your great tolerance and mercy, we would never be able to obtain Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We can never glorify you enough, because if you had not come none of us would be here today. We owe our lives to you. Therefore we should completely surrender our entire lives to your lotus feet. This will qualify us to satisfy the previous *ācāryas* and Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Nothing more is to be gained than this. We are fallen and limited. How can we represent your mission in this society?

Ten years ago, when the Centennial campaign started, we were moved to make a humble attempt to increase your preaching mission in Mira Road (Mumbai). We bought a piece a land, and by your unlimited blessings we are now slowly growing as a new transcendental branch of your ISKCON tree. Now we beg for your compassionate glance so that with your kind supervision this *yātrā* will be able to construct a beautiful temple for their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārījī, with its only purpose being to broadcast the glories of the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Always desiring to please you, we remain  
Your granddisciples and eternal servants in Mira Road, Mumbai, India.

## Nagpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we would like to take the opportunity to describe our main vision for the next year.

In last year’s Vyāsa-pūjā offering we reported that we procured an acre of land and that we would soon be moving near it. It took us no less than four months to renovate the vacant building given to us by Dr. Shyamsundar and make it inhabitable; only then could we transfer to our new locale. Though it is little bit outside the city, all the devotees love this new place. By our getting our own land, our nomadic wandering over more than twenty years has finally ended.

On the day of the move, our congregational members, who all reside on the other side of Nagpur, could not bear to be separated from their beloved Lords, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha, and Their devotees, and so with a heavy heart they accompanied the Deities to Their new residence. Some wept. It was as if Akrūra were taking Kṛṣṇa from Vṛndāvana to Mathurā. This shows how much dedication and affection the Nagpur congregation has for their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha.

Now, with the temple no longer nearby, many members of the congregation are repeatedly requesting us to visit their houses and not totally forget them. We temple devotees also miss our congregation, who are our spiritual family. We have all become very close, enjoying a sweet fraternal relationship that has developed over many years. Even though the temple is now situated so far from them, the congregational devotees never miss coming for *darśana*; this is another sign of their loyalty and devotional enthusiasm to please the Lord. To diminish their feelings of separation, we have involved them in a new program of *nāma-haṭṭa* preaching. In this way the leading initiated devotees among the congregation are engaged in preaching activities, and their grievance at being so far from the temple has lessened.

After the move last year, we started preparing for Janmāṣṭamī. Despite it being the rainy season, we were all ready to take the risk of putting on an elaborate Janmāṣṭamī festival at our new property. We got a large waterproof pandal, and by the mercy of the Lord the program went quite smoothly. This is the first time in the history of ISKCON Nagpur that we could celebrate Janmāṣṭamī on our own land. Another important event that took place in Nagpur this year occurred during the Gaura-pūrṇimā festival. At that time we held the Maharashtra Vaiṣṇava Seminar 2007, which focused on Caitanya *kathā.* Many senior devotees attended this four-day seminar.

At present our plan is to build the new temple and greatly expand our preaching. We know that these have to go side by side. Regarding the temple construction, our architects have almost finalized the design. Now we need to start extensive fund-raising. The cost of the project will be about Rs.100,000,000 ($2,000,000). There will be no problem raising these funds if we are Kṛṣṇa conscious. In your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 7.2.10 you mention that all necessities of life can be easily acquired if we perform austerity, *yajña,* and Vedic study and follow the four regulative principles. The austerity you have given us includes attending *maṅgala-ārati* and performing other elements of *sādhana* to keep us pure and Kṛṣṇa conscious so we can push on the mission. The main *yajña* you gave us is the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. Regarding Vedic study, we thought that our temple can be called “The Temple of Well-being and Learning.” Through the facilities the temple will provide, we hope to help people attain well-being for the body, mind, intelligence, and soul, as well as knowledge and inspiration by systematic study of your books.

In the 1980s one of your dear godbrothers said,

ISKCON will fulfill everyone’s inner demand, even extending to the vegetables and stones, taking them to the feet of Mahāprabhu and the Gosvāmīs. From village to village, everyone, in every place, should be . . . approached and enjoined: ‘Take the name of Kṛṣṇa! Come under the flag of Mahāprabhu!’ This grand, worldwide mission will thrive like anything; it will touch the heavens and cover the Earth and other planets also. That was the aim of Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, and we understand and appreciate that.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have a strong desire to preach and bring everyone here in Nagpur under the magnanimous *avatāra* of Kali-yuga, Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu. Today our prayer is that you please bless us with the intelligence to spread the mission of Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Your faithful servants in Nagpur, India.

## Boise

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet on this most blessed day. All glories to you and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Baṅkebihārī, the presiding Deities of Boise, who so kindly have agreed to reside in Boise because you are here.

Boise ISKCON is primarily a *gṛhastha* temple. All the devotees and aspiring *bhaktas* are householders. Struggling to maintain a balance between our desires, family obligations, and Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we barely get our morning program done before the 8 to 5 routine of serving the material world begins — a routine that ends at night with *prasādam* and family time.

We aspire to do what is closest to your heart — *saṅkīrtana* — but we fall short. We think you wrote the purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 6.3.25 with us in mind:

Especially in this Age of Kali, *saṅkīrtana* alone is sufficient. If the members of our temples in the different parts of the world simply continue *saṅkīrtana* before the Deity, especially before Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, they will remain perfect. There is no need of any other performances. Nevertheless, to keep oneself clean in habits and mind, Deity worship and other regulative principles are required. Śrīla Jīva Gosvāmī says that although *saṅkīrtana* is sufficient for the perfection of life, the *arcanā,* or worship of the Deity in the temple, must continue in order that the devotees may stay clean and pure. Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura therefore recommended that one follow both processes simultaneously. We strictly follow his principle of performing Deity worship and *saṅkīrtana* along parallel lines. This we should continue.

Even in Deity worship we falter. We commit many offenses in our Deity worship every day, and we still find Their Lordships smiling.

It cannot be our service. We seem to be rushing in and out of the *pūjārī* room finding all sorts of shortcuts.

Our offerings are simple and meager. It can’t be our recipes or cooking. It is because of you that The Divine Couple sit down to eat and relish. Even more good news is that They seem to have gained weight!

We figure it must be you, continuously requesting ŚrīmatīRādhārāṇī to ignore our offenses.

Our Lord Jagannātha’s *ratha,* in the past, has been a Chinese Rickshaw, the back of a pickup truck, and a converted hay wagon, and the Lord still went out smiling with His brother Balarāma and sister Subhadrā.

It can’t be the *ratha* or the devotees. It must be because of you that He accepts our ride.

The most munificent Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi make sure that on Gaura Pūrṇimā the temple is miraculously full of first-time guests. Again, it can’t be our *saṅkīrtana*. They are just fulfilling your ardent desire that in every corner of the globe the holy name is given to the conditioned souls.

And even Their Lordships Lakṣmī-Nṛsiṁha appear satisfied. Every child as young as three years old in the temple congregation can sing *namas te narasiṁhāya*. It cannot be that they picked it up from the Star TV channel.

It must be due to your mercy that they can switch from English to Sanskrit and sing the glories of Lord Nṛsiṁha so easily and fluently.

The children of the temple do very well. Several of them sit in our *Bhagavad-gītā* club every Friday evening with the adults and surprise everyone by their understanding of your purports. It cannot be the Boise school system. \[no par\]It is by the power of your purports that young and old alike can grasp the philosophy. It is also the fulfillment of your desire that the third generation take to Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that ISKCON can flourish. Therefore Kṛṣṇa provides them the intelligence.

We are thankful to Boise Biharvan’s Baṅkebihārījī, but we are even more thankful for your incessant mercy on the fallen souls. It is because of you that we exist with whatever little Kṛṣṇa consciousness we have. Please continue to shower your mercy on us, guide us as we *gṛhamedhīs* try to become *gṛhasthas*.

All your servants in New Biharvan, Boise, Idaho, USA.  
(written by Anantarūpa Dāsa)

## New Dvārakā

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine appearance in this material world. We are eternally grateful to you.

As Kali-yuga becomes more and more difficult for devotees to bear, we are protected by remembering your pastimes in this world, which form a wondrous brilliant lamp to light our path through the dark night.

So much information is now available about your divine grace from biographies, stories, letters, films, and photos. It becomes obvious after studying these that you are the loving personification of *bhakti* and that your disciples are completely overwhelmed with this love. You can disarm anyone with your very sweet, gentle nature. On the other hand, one simple retort from you makes heads spin. Your pure love is so appealing that no matter what their pain or sorrow, no matter what their doubt or calculation, those who have the good fortune to know you are flooded by the grace of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Here in New Dvārakā your temple is going on by the power of that pure love and grace. The devotees who manage the temple are impelled to go on in spite of many personal inconveniences, just for the sake of seeing that your instructions are followed and that there remains a solid atmosphere in which to follow you.

New Dvārakā in Los Angeles has been compared to a fresh lotus growing in the midst of a muddy pond. Can anyone explain the mystical presence of a holy place surrounded by Māyās reflection? It thrives due to the power of the holy name and your disciples’ relentless love and sacrifice for you.

Due to this great love, Śrīla Prabhupāda, New Dvārakā is helpful to all kinds of people. There is something spiritual here for everyone. There is the gorgeous Deity worship of Śrī-Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa. There is delicious *prasādam* at Govinda’s Buffet. *Harināma* and/or book distribution parties go out to meet people every day. New Dvārakā is a destination for many students and guests interested in Vedic culture. Your museum is an amazing presentation of the *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is.* Your Govinda’s Imports attracts many shoppers interested in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. There are even celebrities who visit regularly, purchase books, take *prasādam,* and get shelter from their hectic, sometimes difficult lives. The temple broadcasts our morning and evening programs of *kīrtana* and lectures to hundreds of internet viewers.

When Kṛṣṇa consciousness was very new in Los Angeles, the devotees were made to vacate their first address, a Hollywood apartment, and scatter throughout the city. At that time you actually gave Sunday feast lectures from inside a decorated garage. Still your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement became extremely popular here, so you demanded that your disciples find a temple building to give shelter to all of the visitors.

At last some properties were acquired and you named the community New Dvārakā, in honor of the vast property of Śrī Dvārakādhīśa, who made a home out of an entire island in the sea, with 16,108 large palaces. Later, when the devotees inquired if they could name the temple Deities Śrī-Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa, you agreed. After that you referred to Their Lordships as Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa in your speaking and letters to disciples.

At the installation of the Deities you spoke to your eager young disciples, recently moved from garage centers to the first ISKCON-owned property, financed by the sales of your magazines and your own personal *dakṣiṇā*.

*Ye māṁ bhaktyā prayacchati*. Real thing is *bhakti*. What you can offer to Kṛṣṇa? Everything belongs to Kṛṣṇa. What you have got? What is your value? And what is the value of your things? It is nothing. Therefore real thing is *bhaktyā*, real thing is your feeling. ‘Kṛṣṇa, kindly take it. I have no qualification. I am most rotten, fallen, but \[begins to cry\] I have brought this thing for you. Please take it.’ This will be accepted. Don’t be puffed up. Always be careful. You are dealing with Kṛṣṇa. That is my request. Thank you very much.

As you spoke, your voice broke like a tidal wave of love on the great shore of your humble service to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

It seems apparent that Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa worship, or Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa worship, was always foremost in your mind. Your mind was a throne for Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and that is how They came here to us. They were literally transferred from your heart and into our temple. They could not have come to us in any other way. We never had the qualification to receive Them or even recognize Them.

Just as the original Los Angeles disciples received Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa from the throne of your heart, Śrīla Prabhupāda, nowadays those who want to access the mercy of your Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya do so through your advanced followers. Whatever we can hope to know of your pure devotional service can only be received by us from your disciples who are surrendered to following your instructions. That is your sustaining power, which gives the light to guide us in the darkness of this age.

Your humble servants at ISKCON New Dvārakā Dhāma, Los Angeles, California, USA.

## New Ekacakra Dhama

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Daily we can realize your glory, but it is hard to describe it. The longer we are in this movement, the more realizations we get of your glories. And these do not pertain just to our own spiritual lives. We can see the effects of your mercy on common people when they come in contact with you. Slowly these people, who are lost in material, sinful life, are becoming devotees.

We have been placed into a difficult position here, and we are trying to maintain this project as best we can. But we take this difficult condition as Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, and we depend only on your mercy to meet the difficulties.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our situation is such that it is as if we have begun this project anew. And so to remain enthused we read your books, we listen to your lectures, and we read your letters to disciples who faced situations similar to ours. It is real nectar to experience your association and receive your instructions in this way. We are getting a lot of realizations form your instructions in your letters and books, which you have given to everybody. The process works, but it seems difficult because people have so many wrong ideas.

We try to follow your instructions amid changing situations. Your glory, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, is indescribable. Every day we see how you are showering us with mercy. We cannot understand how great your desire is that all living entities become devotees of Kṛṣṇa and go back home, back to Godhead.

The people who visit Govinda’s restaurant are slowly changing into devotees. They think about the purpose of their lives, they read your books, and they thank you for your great mercy. We would be lost without you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You have given meaning to our lives. The regulations, the instructions, your association — all these are helping us progress toward the goal of our lives. You once said that ISKCON is your body, and it is a fact that everyone who has a connection with this organization and begins to follow your instructions gets the dust of your lotus feet.

ISKCON is a great benediction for every living entity, and it provides protection for those who resolve to accept shelter of your lotus feet. And for those who commit themselves body, mind, and soul to spreading your mission, your mercy is unlimited. We have gotten this mercy, and we are still getting it. We all owe you a great debt, which cannot be repaid.

We hope that at least our meager efforts for spreading your mission in this world will make you happy.

The eternal servants of your servants in New Ekacakra Dhāma, Slovakia.  
(written by Mohana Rūpa Dāsa)

## New Gokula

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet!

Feeling incapable of properly expressing our feelings of gratitude for your compassion and love, we beg the previous *ācāryas* to help us as we try to pen a few appropriate words. Coming from the unfortunate category of *nitya-baddha,* we wonder how it will be possible for us to pay proper homage to a *nitya-siddha* Vaiṣṇava guru — Your Divine Grace. However, we will try our best and pray that you will be pleased by our sincere attempt.

In the *Mahābhārata* (*Vana-parva* 313.116), Yamarāja asks his son, Mahārāja Yudhiṣṭhira, “What is the most amazing thing in this world?” Kng Yudhiṣṭhira replies,

*ahany ahani bhūtāni gacchantīha yamālayam*  
*śeṣāḥ sthāvaram icchanti kim āścaryam ataḥ param*

**Day after day, countless living entities in this world go to the kingdom of death. Still, those who remain aspire for a permanent situation here. What could be more amazing than this?**

Having been deceived by the dazzling display of *māyā-śakti*, we have forgotten our eternal relationship with Rādhā-Govinda and have accepted Māyās home as our own. However, we are now running away from her, fervently begging with tears in our eyes, “O Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please do not leave us stranded here bereft of your loving shelter! Please do not give up on us! O ocean of mercy and friend of the fallen, please give us eternal shelter in the shade of your lotus feet!”

Consisting of impenetrable layers of matter, and guarded by karmic laws, this universal prison house, *brahmāṇḍa,* is unquestionably impossible to escape . . . unless you give us your mercy.

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**According to their karma, all living entities are wandering throughout the entire universe. Some of them are being elevated to the upper planetary systems, and some are going down into the lower planetary systems. Out of many millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets an opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. By the mercy of both Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, such a person receives the seed of the creeper of devotional service. \[*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta*****,** ***Madhya-līlā*** **19.151\]**

Those fortunate souls who have received such mercy willingly accept all kinds of adverse conditions while remaining in the material world, as stated in the Fourth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (4.30.33), wherein the Pracetās pray to the Lord:

*yāvat te māyayā spṛṣṭā bhramāma iha karmabhiḥ*  
*tāvad bhavat-prasaṅgānāṁ saṅgaḥ syān no bhave bhave*

**Dear Lord, as long as we have to remain within this material world due to our material contamination and wander from one type of body to another and from one planet to another, we pray that we may associate with those who are engaged in discussing Your pastimes. We pray for this benediction life after life, in different bodily forms and on different planets.**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said:

Life is very transient. At any moment we can die. Therefore those who are advanced devotee, they want to see that “I have got very short period of life at my disposal.” Therefore, he is very anxious to utilize every moment for advancing in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That is *madhyama-adhikārī*. \[Lecture in Bombay, 5 December 1974\]

To help us advance in devotional service, you have instituted ISKCON, explained the most advanced Vaiṣṇava philosophy, connected us to the Godhead through a bona fide lineage, and, most importantly, selflessly given us your loving guidance and perfect example.

It is an unimaginable great fortune that we can grasp even an iota of the nectar of transcendental taste in the service of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. However, this is made possible only by following your divine instructions through *bhakti-sādhana*, and properly serving the holy name.

*nāma cintāmaṇiḥ kṛṣṇaś caitanya-rasa-vigrahaḥ*  
*pūrṇaḥśuddho nitya-mukto ’bhinnatvān nāma-nāminoḥ*

**The holy name of Kṛṣṇa is transcendentally blissful. It bestows all spiritual benedictions, for it is Kṛṣṇa Himself, the reservoir of all pleasure. Kṛṣṇa’s name is complete, and it is the form of all transcendental mellows. It is not a material name under any condition, and it is no less powerful than Kṛṣṇa Himself. Since Kṛṣṇa’s name is not contaminated by the material qualities, there is no question of its being involved with** ***māyā*****. Kṛṣṇa’s name is always liberated and spiritual; it is never conditioned by the laws of material nature. This is because the name of Kṛṣṇa and Kṛṣṇa Himself are identical. \[*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta*****,** ***Madhya-līlā*** **17.133\]**

Thank you for this matchless gift, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Thank you for everything!

Your aspiring servants in New Gokula Dhāma, Vancouver, Canada.  
(written by Bhāva Dāsa)

## New Goloka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. We come together every year on this auspicious day to celebrate the glorious anniversary of your appearance in this dark and troubled material world. On this day over a century ago, a ray of Lord Caitanya’s mercy shone forth from the home of Gour Mohan and Rajani De in Calcutta. From that moment you were destined to take the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Caitanya Mahāprabhu all over the world.

You were especially merciful to the fallen and spoiled youth of America in the 1960s when you came to New York with barely five dollars in your pocket, but with the invaluable treasure of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s holy name in your heart. Empowered by the instructions of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja, you were able to do what no other Vaiṣṇava had ever done — *fulfill the prophecy of Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura that the holy names of the Lord would be chanted all around the world!* By the time you left this world in 1977, millions and millions of people on all continents had heard and were chanting

*Hare Kṛṣṇa Hare Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rāma Hare Rāma Rāma Rāma Hare Hare*

We, your humble servants at New Goloka *dhāma*, offer our heartfelt gratitude for the mercy you have bestowed upon us by allowing us to participate in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. Year in and year out we are blessed with your association through your books, CDs, and DVDs. You have also blessed us with our ISKCON family, where we can associate with other Vaiṣṇavas in the presence of their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda. There is no way that we can ever repay you for your gift of devotional service. But we pray that we continue to be engaged in your service so that we can further assist you in spreading the glories of pure devotional service to the suffering conditioned souls in this world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us with the desire to continue hearing and chanting Kṛṣṇa’s holy names, along with the desire to continue your mission of taking Kṛṣṇa consciousness to every town and village of the world. In this way we may strive to repay you for the unlimited mercy you have bestowed upon us.

Your humble servants at New Goloka Dhāma, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.

## New Māyāpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. It has been thirty-one years now since you visited the French *yātrā* and installed Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma in New Māyāpur.

Last year we celebrated these events, as it was the thirtieth anniversary of the installation ceremony. Devotees flew from far-away places to congregate and share the nectar of remembering those sacred moments in your transcendental association, hearing blissful topics of *kṛṣṇa-kathā* directly from your lotus mouth.

For many devotees it was the first time they had met their spiritual master. Until then you had spoken to them only through your books. Your appearance as the person *bhāgavata,* as well as your direct instructions, shook mountains of doubts down to dust. I personally witnessed how you melted the hearts of all present and how simply by your appearance and powerful words all our material problems shrunk to insignificance.

First of all you challenged:

Is there any problem. Hmm? Is there any difficulty? To see God, is there any difficulty? Every day you see the sun. So Kṛṣṇa says “I am the light of the sun.” So is there any difficulty to see Kṛṣṇa? Every day you drink water . . . no? Everybody drinks water. So this nice quenching taste of water which vanquishes thirst, that is also Kṛṣṇa. So like this you can practice seeing Kṛṣṇa everywhere.

We all rejoiced to realize that the experience of Kṛṣṇa consciousness was so direct and so natural.

Then you gave instructions on how to be happy by living a simple life of devotion dedicated to the Absolute Truth, Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. You told us how to develop New Māyāpur and raise our families, educate our children, grow our own food, and live a perfect life in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Above all, you stressed that we cooperate in a humble mood of self-dedication. It is now thirty-one years since that time!

I am writing this humble offering at your lotus feet from New Māyāpur, under the soothing moonlike smile of Lord Balarāma, as He seems to whisper softly: “Be patient, chant, chant, chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.”

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this beautiful transcendental place, New Māyāpur. We all pray at your lotus feet that it will develop into the spiritual village you envisioned.

Always praying for the sweet rain of your mercy, we beg to remain always engaged in your service at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaura, Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda-Mādhava.

Your servants at New Māyāpur, France.  
(written by Ādiśekhara Dāsa)

## New Navadwipa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept on this glorious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration our humble obeisances.

Alone and without fuss or fanfare, you came to the incorrigibly decadent West and told whoever would listen that they had got it all wrong. That our happiness was actually misery; that our friends, our society, and our loves were all illusory relationships without meaning; and fundamentally that we were falsely identifying with an inert bag of mucus, bile, and stool in a dream world of our own imagination.

You gave us an option that transcends our sensory predilections for the gross and subtle objects of matter and identified the real object of our love and the source of unlimited happiness for every living entity — Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Thus we have been blessed by you with the opportunity to replace the chronic problems of birth and death with eternal happiness, in the sure knowledge that Lord Kṛṣṇa is the infallible ultimate shelter and the only truly worthy recipient of our unmotivated and undeviating affection and devotion.

For this we have incurred a debt to you and to your loyal followers who have faithfully represented and explained your teachings. Only a fool thinks he can repay this debt, a debt so profound and immeasurable that acknowledgement of it must include recognition of your fearless repose and your infinite and unfathomable glories, consigning to dust by comparison the greatest fanciful heroes of mundane history.

Our concern, at least for those who are genuinely serious about solving the problems of birth and death, must be primarily for the health of our spiritual creepers. Are our knowledge, taste, and enthusiasm continuing with progressive purity and freedom from the effects of fructifying weeds within our hearts? Despite the flood of politically correct thinking, counseling, legislative arrangements, economic arrangements, genuine concern for our physical well-being, and many other well-intentioned endeavors, is our faith still focused on that supreme and ultimate goal of all sentient endeavors, love for Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa?

In this, our only real self-interest, we would be failing in sincerity and honesty if we did not unreservedly fall repeatedly at your feet and pray for your help and the pure desire to follow with the greatest fidelity your instructions. Who except the most hopeless of us will be content with anything less than a conclusive and unequivocal understanding of your instructions and how best to follow them? Failing to properly understand eventually and inevitably leads to the corrosion and possible devastation of our respectful, friendly, and affectionate dealings with Vaiṣṇavas. In this matter we must fervently pray for your blessings and acknowledge the otherwise hopeless, confused, and bewildered nature of our existence.

Your eternal servants at New Navadvīpa Dhāma, Honolulu, Hawaii, USA.  
(written by Maitreya Dāsa)

## New Nilācala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,
