Please accept our sincere obeisances on this occasion of your momentous appearance day. All glories to you and your steadfast service to Guru and Gaurāṅga!

On the order of your spiritual master, you faithfully and courageously carried Lord Caitanya’s mission beyond the borders of Bharata and into the Western world. You introduced many of us to Kṛṣṇa and you taught all of us the means and necessity of approaching Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, as our only true shelter. Your love for your spiritual master and for Kṛṣṇa has given us all the opportunity to at last break free from the suffering of this material world and return to our constitutional loving relationship with the Absolute Truth. For this highest gift, we are forever in your debt.

Glorification of your pure love for Kṛṣṇa and your unprecedented activities never becomes dull, Śrīla Prabhupāda. In no way do we tire of hearing of your character and pastimes. However, having come into contact with your movement, we all have the opportunity to venerate you even more by authentically acting upon your words, following your example, and taking your instructions as our life and soul.

With this in mind, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we in New Nīlācala Dhāma beg for your guidance and enthusiasm to increase the sincerity and magnitude of our chanting of the holy names, both in our *japa* and *kīrtana*; we beg for your instruction and motivation to cultivate genuine devotion while caring for our Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śaradvihārī, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, Subhadrā, and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi; we beg for inspiration to follow your example in how to welcome and attend to our guests and to impress upon those who we visit in the community the supreme character of Kṛṣṇa; we beg most of all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your blessings that we, as a congregation, may continue to deepen our relationships with one another, keeping your desires in the center. In this way we can continue to enthusiastically increase our own Kṛṣṇa consciousness and distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others in the mood of Lord Caitanya, as a service to His mission.

Your aspiring servants at New Nīlācala Dhāma, Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, USA.

## New Pānihāṭi

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this 111thanniversary of your appearance we are meditating on how you have manifested this place of pilgrimage, New Pānihāṭi Dhāma, in Atlanta, Georgia, USA. It is stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.13.10):

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni svāntaḥ-sthena gadābhṛtā*

**My Lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage.**

You further clarify in your purport to this verse,

So by their actions the pure devotees of the Lord can render any place into a place of pilgrimage, and the holy places are worth the name only on their account. Such pure devotees are able to rectify the polluted atmosphere of any place.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya-līlā* 20.57) you describe that since an advanced devotee carries Lord Viṣṇu within his heart, he is a “moving temple and a moving Viṣṇu.” In the beginning of my devotional service, my realization about your naming of the temples in the West after various holy places in India was nil. It seemed like an arbitrary act. Now that I have been serving Your Divine Grace for some time, I can see how it is possible that each of these places is a manifestation of the *līlā* of its namesake in India. Anyone who serves in one of these places for a number of years will experience this realization, by your grace.

You gave one of the shortest arrival lectures ever when you visited New Pānihāṭi Dhāma in 1975. You were sitting on the newly made *vyāsāsana,* drinking in the nectarean beauty of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, the three-foot tall brass Deities that preside over our temple and who stand in a loving, dancing pose. You said that Lord Kṛṣṇa demands that we first surrender and then He will give us love of Godhead. But then you commented on how these two Lords, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, don’t demand anything. Full of transcendental emotion, unable to speak because of a voice choked with love, you ended the address with “so take shelter of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and be happy” and left the temple room.

I have been serving at your New Pānihāṭi Dhāma since 1994. It is by the mercy of this holy place that I and many other devotees have been able to save ourselves from the clutches of Māyā. This salvation has been possible by the extra mercy of this holy place. Pānihāṭi is the city where Lord Nityānanda manifested his mercy on Raghunātha Dāsa Gosvāmī. Lord Nityānanda punished him by ordering him to feed chipped rice and yogurt to the assembled devotees. Lord Nityānanda is so merciful that when Lord Caitanya rejected His first servant, Kālā Kṛṣṇadāsa, for being allured by gypsies, Lord Nityānanda personally took charge of the servant and got Caitanya Mahāprabhu to allow Kṛṣṇadāsa to be engaged in further service. The pastime of Jagāi and Mādhāi is another example of Lord Nityānanda’s mercy. Therefore, it is evident that Lord Nityānanda takes special care of those who are the most fallen. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for establishing this holy place of mercy where the most fallen devotees may revive their devotional service.

We have been annually celebrating the chipped rice, or *dadhi-ciḍā*, pastime of Lord Nityānanda for many years now here in New Pānihāṭi Dhāma. The festival is well attended by devotees from all over the southeastern USA. We pray for your mercy so that we can expand this festival citywide so that nondevotees may take advantage of Lord Nityānanda’s mercy as well. We are trying to maintain the Deity worship, some preaching, and a little book distribution. We pray that you give us the strength to restore the preaching and Deity worship to the levels they had reached when you were present in 1975. Your direct disciples are getting older and looking toward moving on to other services, and your granddisciples, more youthful and with increasing experience, are taking up more responsibilities in the temple. Please give them extra blessings to push on your movement in the Atlanta area. Māyā and Kali are always ready to attack, and we are sometimes bewildered by their tricks. Please continue to strengthen us as we continue to fulfill your order to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Atlanta area.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day, we thank you for that which we can never repay: engagement in your *nitya-līlā* of pure devotional service. Please bless us with the spiritual strength to transform ourselves and the entire city of Atlanta into a holy place of pilgrimage.

Your servants in New Pānihāṭi Dhāma, Atlanta, Georgia, USA.  
(written by Śikhi Māhiti Dāsa)

## New Ramaṇ Reti

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Here in Alachua we are filling up a big room in the house you have built, “in which the whole world can live.” And how grateful we are to Your Divine Grace!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are watching the ISKCON family happily grow around the worship of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara and Gaura-Nitāi. How thankful we are to Your Divine Grace!

You have blessed us with the most sublime transcendental truth and the satisfying eternal engagement of *bhakti,* loving devotional service to the Lord. How indebted we are to Your divine Grace! To repay this debt is our life’s quest, and this year we would like to serve you better. You have emphasized that we should always try to increase. So we will try our best to expand all our projects here in New Ramaṇ Reti. In doing so, the struggles will be our enjoyment, the endeavors will be our strength, and the failures will be our learning. Please accept this small payment.

We have seen what we took to be a picture of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa admiring Themselves in a mirror, so pleased at what They see. However, we have been told that it is not a mirror they are looking at but a picture of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and that They are expressing the unlimited pleasure you have given Them.

We are amazed when we reflect on your exceptional service: your selflessness and compassion, the sacrifice of your own peaceful devotional *samādhi* in Vṛndāvana, and your patience and tolerance with our immaturity. We pray to imbibe your example so we can push ourselves forward to try to please you, as you have pleased Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. You are a hard act to follow, but we promise to stay committed in our tiny attempt.

Thank you for another year of being able to take refuge in your divine house of ISKCON.

Your servants at New Ramaṇ Reti, Alachua, Florida, USA.

## Toronto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, we want to express some of the love and gratitude we feel toward you. Your mercy is all that we are made of. Before meeting you, either directly or through your books and disciples, we were all totally lost and floundering in the ocean of nescience. You came to teach us how to have real relationships based on loving Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. You taught us that we are not this body but are eternal spiritual beings with an eternal relationship with Kṛṣṇa that we had forgotten for untold eons. By your shining example you showed us what could be done by one person who was totally sold out to his spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa. Any good qualities developing in us are only by your incredible compassion.

You sometimes said that Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta was sending devotees to help you in your mission to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We can see that you are sending so many wonderful devotees to take up your mission and spread love of Kṛṣṇa more and more strongly to every town and village. Looking at ISKCON these days, we see so many enthusiastic devotees working together to care for and enliven each other in your service.

Presently we are also witnessing the departure of many of your direct disciples, thus impressing upon us the urgency to more seriously take up your instructions. In this way we can become better instruments to help spread love of Kṛṣṇa to all the unfortunate souls of Kali-yuga. If we become pure, then your message will shine through us as light pours in through a clean window.

Please pray to Kṛṣṇa to protect us from Māyās attractions and keep us safely and eternally at your lotus feet.

Your servants at New Remuṇā Dhāma, Toronto, Canada.

## New Tālavana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. You’ve heard thousands of Vyāsa-pūjā offerings over the last forty years. Certainly your interest remains keen in listen to your disciples express their hearts, give reports, and speak philosophically on the basis of *śāstra*. Therefore on behalf of your disciples and followers at ISKCON in Mississippi, I am humbled by the good fortune to compose our Vyāsa-pūjā offering for the year 2007.

Our hearts are pained by our failure to sufficiently spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in this part of the world. Yet there is an effulgent seed that gives us hope. We pray to you to help us surrender to your service so that this seed might expand beyond our imaginations. Help us to be determined to help others to be Kṛṣṇa conscious. Bless us again and again to spread the holy names throughout this area and teach us to use whatever resources and ingenuity we have to accomplish this.

Our desire and determination to please you comprise this effulgent seed. We want to cultivate this seed and watch it grow. Our desire and determination to serve you must be carefully nurtured and protected. May the Supreme Lord, Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa, enthuse and help us to fulfill your desire to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all.

May our whole being surrender onto your lotus feet, which captivate our minds a hundred percent. May our words articulate the teachings you’ve taught for all to hear, and may our chests, throats, and heads vibrate the *mantras* you’ve given us to chant.

May we never deviate from your society, and may we leave this world a member in good standing. May we meet the challenges before us as you met them: fearlessly in pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And may we lead others back home, back to Godhead, just as you’ve led us and are still leading the world through your books and ISKCON.

May we help your society flourish in the brahminical field of activity. May we always represent you purely, just as you always perfectly represented Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Prabhupāda and the previous *ācāryas*.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please do not give up hope for our success in spiritual life. Please continue to encourage us daily through your devotional perfection. Allow us an eternal place in your International Society for Krishna Consciousness and make us the servants of your servants eternally.

Your servants at New Tālavana, Mississippi, USA.

## Auckland (New Varsana)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Another year has passed by, during which there were so many opportunities for service and realizations. Each year the realizations become deeper:

1\. We are not the body.

2\. We have so many attachments.

3\. So-called material pleasures are temporary.

4\. There is no real love, only lust, in the material world

Each day we experience the tests of *māyā*. We don’t belong here. We belong with Kṛṣṇa in Goloka Vṛndāvana. Nothing really changes. Things go on pretty much the same as the year before — more service, more realizations — until we become self-realized in our relationship with Kṛṣṇa and go back to Godhead and have:

A spiritual, *sac-cid-ānanda* body,  
Attachment only for you,  
Experience of real pleasure,  
An understanding that there is no true love here,  
No more *māyā,*  
A desire to be always be with you.

Now that would be something different and achievable if we wanted it.

All you set out to do, Śrīla Prabhupāda, has been partly or fully achieved:

- Book distribution
- Temple construction
- Deity worship
- *Harināma-saṅkīrtana*
- *Prasādam* distribution
- BBT publications
- TV programs
- Radio programs
- Nāma Haṭṭa preaching
- University preaching
- Making devotees

You have also left us lots of service to be started or completed, like farms, looking after devotees, becoming more prominent in society, etc. The list goes on. It never stops. Pick a service, try our best, and leave the result up to you and Kṛṣṇa.

When someone does something good, we have to acknowledge it and be grateful. So thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as always, for allowing us to do some service in your ISKCON.

You know our hearts and the desires that lie within, mainly to enjoy separately from Kṛṣṇa. We know that if we follow the process, with your help we can purify these desires, and this will make you happy. This is the goal of our service, pleasing the guru. Your relationship with us is like that of a loving father, always very patient.

We can go on appreciating you because thinking of you always brings realization of your greatness and dedication to your Guru Mahārāja.

Praying that we can continue for another year, we remain  
Your servants in New Varṣāṇā, Auckland, New Zealand.  
(written by Kalasamvara Dāsa)

## New Vraja-dhāma (Hungary)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please, accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you!

When Lord Kṛṣṇa appears in this material world He does so for different reasons. In the *Bhagavad-gītā* He tells Arjuna why He comes here:

*paritrāṇāya sādhūnāṁ vināśāya ca duṣkṛtām*  
*dharma-saṁsthāpanārthāya sambhavāmi yuge yuge*

**To deliver the pious and to annihilate the miscreants, as well as to reestablish the principles of religion, I Myself appear, millennium after millennium. \[*****Bhagavad-gītā*** **4.8\]**

In this Age of Kali the Lord appeared as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to spread His *saṅkīrtana* movement. This *avatāra* of the Lord does not kill mischievous people, but by His causeless mercy He delivers them. This causeless mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu was manifested by you all over the world. By your mercy the congregational chanting of the holy name has spread on all the continents, and thus you have made it possible for everyone to attain pure love of the Lord.

When Lord Kṛṣṇa was present He annihilated vast number of demons. However, at the Battlefield of Kurukṣetra He entrusted this task to Arjuna. Although the Lord Himself could have killed these demons, He wanted to give the credit to Arjuna.

Similarly, Lord Caitanya could have spread this *saṅkīrtana* movement all over the world, but he entrusted this mission to you so that you would get all the credit. As Arjuna is crowned with glory for the battle at Kurukṣetra, even after such a long time, you are similarly crowned with eternal glory for spreading *harināma saṅkīrtana* throughout the world.

The splendor of your glory is not fading; on the contrary, the more that people chant the holy names, the brighter and more effulgent it becomes.

Arjuna killed lots of demons. In the same way, you have destroyed the demoniac mentality of a large number of people. Even at present many people are giving up their demoniac way of thinking due to your books, recordings, and your followers’ preaching.

While wandering in South India Lord Caitanya gave Kūrma *brāhmaṇa* the following instruction:

*yāre dekha, tāre kaha ‘kṛṣṇa’-upadeśa*  
*āmāra ājñāya guru hañā tāra’ ei deśa*

**Instruct everyone to follow the orders of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as they are given in the** ***Bhagavad-gītā*** **and** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*****. In this way become a spiritual master and try to liberate everyone in this land. \[*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā*** **7.128\]**

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura also entrusted you with this mission, and through your own example you taught this to us as well.

Through your books you showed the world the mood and practice of pure devotion. At the same time, you established large numbers of temples and farm communities, which exist as spiritual islands in the ocean of material ignorance.

Under the guidance of your dear disciple Śivarāma Swami, several spiritual islands like these were established here in Hungary too, according to your instructions.

New Vraja Dhāma is a farm community that was founded fourteen years ago to establish *varṇāśrama-dharma*. Our efforts are aimed at bringing satisfaction to you and thus getting your blessings.

We are continuously distributing your books, which bring about serious changes in people’s lives. Their hearts change to such an extent that they become curious about what Kṛṣṇa consciousness is and visit the realm of Rādhā-Śyāmasundara. When they come to New Vraja Dhāma, they become acquainted with the basic principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, take *prasādam,* and even perform some *kīrtana* in front of the Deities. After that they are also shown the practice of simple living and high thinking. We introduce people to the importance and practical application of cow protection. A lot of them leave this place with the feeling that it has been the most wonderful day of their lives. Many people become really inquisitive about Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

This is what you gave the world. You showed how people even with a demoniac mentality can be brought closer and closer to the ultimate goal of life. Thank you for all that you did for us and the world. We hope that our insignificant efforts can bring some satisfaction to you.

We would like to ask for your blessings that New Vraja Dhāma can function as a successful example of *varṇāśrama-dharma*, in preaching, cow protection, and also practical application of simple living and high thinking. With our humble endeavors we would like to satisfy you and the *guru-paramparā.*

On behalf of our community, I offer all our deeds to the service of your lotus feet and pray for your mercy so that your transcendental vision will be manifest here.

Your servants at New Vraja Dhāma, Hungary.  
(written by Rādhā-kānta Dāsa)

## New Vṛndāvana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

On this auspicious day of your appearance, remembering your transcendental activities and amazing accomplishments is always simply wonderful for us. Even more marvelous and awe-inspiring are the glorious visions created by your words! We find it so easy and comfortable to glorify you on this day, offer some flowers, and just chant *Jaya Jaya* Prabhupāda!

What we are learning is that to manifest even one of your visions into a reality is challenging every ounce of our collective intelligence, taking every bit of our strength, and costing us our very lives! The service you have offered us — developing New Vṛndāvana, a replica of the original Vṛndāvana — is your great mercy upon us.

The landscape of New Vṛndāvana is forever sanctified and transformed by the sprinkling of the dust of your lotus feet during your four visits here. Since then, we have been taken on a relentless roller-coaster ride of euphoric highs as well as depressive lows. Just by following your instructions, devotees became empowered and achieved miraculous results! Disobeying you produced nothing but miserable, deviant failures. We have lived through the excitement of riding a unique wave of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement and believing we could accomplish anything. We have also experienced the frightening nightmare of not knowing if the community would even survive another day.

The ever-broadening scope of the project — from its inception as a very sweet, personal Kṛṣṇa conscious, self-sufficient farming/cowherd village, expanding into a dynamic replica of Vṛndāvana, replete with seven opulent temples, and then exploding into a national *tīrtha, the* pilgrimage place in North America — has inflicted on the community a serious identity crisis.

How to go forward, how to prioritize, plan, and inspire a vision that will excite and unite the devotees and congregation? How to take care of all the routine work, how to maintain a decaying infrastructure, how to keep our heads out of all the legal nooses? How to spiritually and practically care for residents so they will be encouraged to live a life of hearing and chanting in the association of devotees, and to die with dignity, enthusiastically pursuing *kṛṣṇa-prema* up until their last breath?

Any idea we may have had of building New Vṛndāvana by bricks, mortar, concrete, steel, and marble was simply childish! The understanding of Vṛndāvana culture, where loving Kṛṣṇa is the life force of the Brijabāsīs, where pure devotional service is the only activity, and where perfect harmony exists between all residents of the *dhāma,* is a spiritual achievement that has eluded us thus far.

You wrote to us in 1968 that manifesting New Vṛndāvana is not a material affair but can be accomplished only by the pure chanting of the holy name. It is that instruction that we wish to rededicate ourselves to on this most holy day of your appearance.

Your servants at the New Vṛndāvana Community, West Virginia, USA.

## New York (Brooklyn)

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I offer my respectful obeisance’s unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most blessed occasion of your transcendental Vyāsa-pūjā*mahā-mahotsava*! Please bless all the devotees with your mercy and love, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for without your blessings, inspiration, and support on the path of devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa, our progress is most difficult. Your Divine Grace describes this wonderful Kṛṣṇa consciousness process so nicely, and your message is easy for even a simple and foolish person to understand:

The word *guru-prasāda* indicates that the spiritual master is very merciful in bestowing the boon of devotional service upon the disciple. That is the best possible gift the spiritual master has to offer. Those with a background of pious life are eligible to receive life’s supreme benefit, and to bestow this benefit, the Supreme Personality of Godhead sends His representative to impart His mercy. Endowed with the mercy of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the spiritual master distributes the mercy to those who are elevated and pious. Thus the spiritual master trains his disciples to render devotional service unto the Supreme Personality of Godhead. This is called *guru-kṛpā*. It is *kṛṣṇa-prasāda,* Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, that He sends a bona fide spiritual master to the deserving disciple. By the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, one meets the bona fide spiritual master, and by the mercy of the spiritual master, the disciple is fully trained in the devotional service of the Lord. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 19.152, purport\]

Being unfortunate and insignificant beggars at your door, we present our humble plea, Śrīla Prabhupāda: please be merciful to us, please make devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa always available to us in abundance.

But how long shall we be beggars at your door, Śrīla Prabhupāda? When will we join together and be givers instead of takers and bear wonderful gifts for your transcendental pleasure? When oh when, Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we learn to accept your mercy, which is always flowing from Your Divine Grace and which is the ultimate mercy of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajīs ever-increasing reservoir of bliss and love, delivered to fill up our hearts’ void, created by impersonalism, with the absolute sacrament of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*?

In the summer of 1966 Your Divine Grace became the first, most wonderful, and best member of ISKCON, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. As the first and foremost member of ISKCON, you hold title to a position no other member can claim: Founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. As a self-realized pure devotee and direct representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, you guide all those who contact Your Divine Grace by hearing the *śabda-brahman,* the pure transcendental Kṛṣṇa conscious sound vibrating forever from the mystical Bhaktivedanta purports.

That ecstatic wisdom of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is presented so nicely in your transcendental books — a wisdom that remains hidden and is willing to appear only out of reciprocation to the sincere soul, surrendered to accepting your gift of mercy and love, the matchless gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you always offer freely, but somehow or other the very same gift we, due to our inadequacies, fail to see and repeatedly and shamelessly beg for in blindness. Your pure and perfect example of Kṛṣṇa consciousness has set the standard we have come to expect for a fully self-realized pure devotee of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, a *mahā-bhāgavata* devotee of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, and a disciplic-succession *ācārya* representing Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we conclude that you are indeed the one and only founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. On that July day in 1966, at the Matchless Gifts storefront, the first Hare Kṛṣṇa temple in the Western world, Your Divine Grace established the mission statement of this wonderful and great International Society for Krishna Consciousness, ISKCON, which you likened to your body. Hearing and chanting the holy name and transcendental pastimes of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa certainly constitute the essence of the original ISKCON mission, and therefore with enthusiasm, determination, much new energy, and teamwork, a growing group of sincere devotees, very eager to serve the ISKCON mission Your Divine Grace established to fulfill the desire of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, have taken up the potent spirit of *harināma saṅkīrtana* and the distribution of your transcendental books, which are also Lord Kṛṣṇa’s books, simply to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and also to help the unfortunate souls receive this wonderful opportunity to taste the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, for which they really hanker. The New York ISKCON Monthly Saṅkīrtana Festival is a combined effort of young and older devotees, congregational and temple *āśrama* devotees — all your sincere servants, Śrīla Prabhupāda, all deserving of your mercy and love, Śrīla Prabhupāda, all concerned with fulfilling the mandate of your most important spiritual mission, to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness, by undertaking to perform a wonderful endeavor and offer the greatest sacrifice. Those wonderful devotees truly offer a wonderful homage to you today, a wonderful gift to Your Divine Grace on this most blessed occasion. We hope you are pleased with this humble gift, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We hope you will allow your merciful and loving smile of approval to be revealed. But we need your special mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, since we are aimlessly and foolishly wandering in darkness. We are blind. We cannot see, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Our only vision, our only light, our only hope, and our only goal is to make your mission our mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and . . .

To systematically propagate spiritual knowledge to society at large and to educate all peoples in the techniques of spiritual life in order to check the imbalance of values in life and to achieve real unity and peace in the world.

To propagate a consciousness of Krishna as it is revealed in the Bhagawat Gita and Srimad Bhagawatam.

To bring the members of the Society together with each other and nearer to Krishna, the prime entity, and thus to develop the idea within the members and humanity at large that each soul is part and parcel of the quality of Godhead (Krishna).

To teach and encourage the Samkirtan movement, congregational chanting of the holy name of God, as revealed in the teachings of Lord Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu.

To erect for the members and for society at large a holy place of transcendental pastimes dedicated to the personality of Krishna.

To bring the members closer together for the purpose of teaching a simpler and more natural way of life.

With a view towards achieving the aforementioned purposes, to publish and distribute periodicals, magazines, books and other writings.

We beg to remain your servants forever, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your insignificant servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandira in New York City, New York, USA.  
(written by Rāmabhadra Dāsa)

## Nitāi Gaurāṅga Āśrama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O grand–spiritual-master Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a great, merciful soul. You traveled around the world, taking Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa with you. Your transcendental activities, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are like a huge earthquake that shakes the whole world, or like a big tsunami that washes away all the *māyā.*

All over the world, countless living entities have received your causeless mercy. In this millennium, there is no one greater or more powerful than you. You have perfectly preached the *yuga-dharma* for this Age of Kali, the chanting of the holy names of Kṛṣṇa.

You will always be glorified in this world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*! *Oṁ tat sat*.

Your servants at Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga Āśrama, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Vidura Dāsa)

## New Delhi (Noida)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet! Hare Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā day!

All glories to the revolutionary movement you started formally in 1966 as the International Society for Krishna Consciousness!

As the pastimes of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu are like an ocean, so the pastimes of His confidential servitor — you, Śrīla Prabhupāda — are also oceanic. As the pastimes and activities of the Supreme Lord are pleasing to hear, so the pastimes performed by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are very dear to the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, are also pleasing to hear.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, what can we offer unto your lotus feet on this, your very special appearance day, when whatever we have has been received from you alone? It is as you would often say: “When we wish to make an offering to mother Ganges, we take a palmful of water from the Ganges and offer it back to the Ganges.” Today, Śrīla Prabhupāda, whatever little attempt we are making as your Vyāsa-pūjā offering, you will notice that it consists only of what we have learned from you, through your books, recordings, and sincere disciples.

You have established an organization where the sky is the limit for learning. With the help of training programs set in motion by you within ISKCON, people are learning such subjects as civilization, spirituality, the art of preaching, Deity worship, building construction, negotiating skills, computers, managerial skills, financial management, speaking skills, organizational skills, cooking, singing, chanting, dancing, conflict resolution, etc. They are even learning the art of dying. In all these subjects the central point is that everything should be used to please the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Thus devotees are using all these skills to push on the divine movement you founded in order to distribute love of Kṛṣṇa to everyone.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here at your new project, ISKCON Noida, consisting of the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda temple and the Bhaktivedanta Academy for spiritual science, we are under the expert guidance of your sincere disciples His Holiness Gopāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami and His Holiness Lokanāth Swami. The construction is progressing nicely, and the project is developing along with it. Our whole purpose is to fulfill your desire to establish systematic spiritual educational programs. The special feature of ISKCON Noida comprises school programs, where hundreds and hundreds of schoolchildren are introduced to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. In addition, the temple programs are going on regularly, along with weekly *harināmas,* daily Food for Life *prasādam* distribution for schoolchildren, book distribution, and home programs.

This year we introduced the Bhakti Vriksha program after training some congregational devotees to take it up. By your blessings, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we now have a few branches of this *bhakti* tree in Noida, and many more will follow in the near future. You have been so kind to give us various programs for our spiritual progress. Also, we have introduced the *śikṣā* program in the temple to cultivate the masses. Śrīla Prabhupāda, more than one hundred new devotees have enrolled in this *śikṣā* program and taken vows in the presence of Deities to begin their devotional lives and consistently follow the prescribed standards at home. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your simple formula of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness by giving people nice *prasādam,* sweet *kīrtana,* and *kṛṣṇa-kathā* is still working. Now, by your mercy, the congregational devotees are enthusiastically attending temple programs, preaching, going out on book distribution, and organizing festivals and educational programs.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have kindly given us this new project at Noida. Now please give us the intelligence not only to complete it but also to expertly help many more souls go back to Godhead. You have so expertly given us all the temple programs. Now please give us the strength and wisdom to respect them by regularly attending them in the association of devotees. Please bestow upon us your causeless mercy so that we can forget about our contaminated false egos and start co-operating. In this way we can become insignificant instruments in your preaching mission and understand this essential instruction of yours: “United we stand, divided we fall.”

Hare Kṛṣṇa.

The servants of the servants of your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda mandir and Bhaktivedanta Academy for Spiritual Science, Noida, New Delhi, India.  
(written by Vraja Bhakti Vilāsa Dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on your divine appearance day!

In glorifying you, the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, we must take into consideration your tremendous contribution to the mission of Lord Caitanya: *pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma, sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*. \[*CB Antya-khaṇḍa* 4.126\] Recently, here in Pandharpur-dhāma, I realized that your greatest contribution is your amazing books, which can benefit every human being. And what may be more amazing is that some of your disciples have imbibed the essence of your books and made their lives a testament to your greatness.

Last night I witnessed this second amazing phenomenon when your dear disciple Janānanda Goswami (who received *sannyāsa dīkṣā* from Lokanāth Swami on Gaura Pūrṇimā day) performed one of the dramas he is famous for: “The Genie in the Bottle.” You know the story, Śrīla Prabhupāda. . . .

A king sends his minister to a market to purchase everything still unsold. The minister approaches an old man (I played the old man) and tries to purchase a beautiful ornate exotic lamp from him. The old man refuses on the grounds that it is too dangerous: The lantern contains a genie who can grant any desire, but if left unengaged he kills the lamp’s owner.

Even after being repeatedly warned of the lethal hazards, the minister insists. “O.K. I will sell you the bottle with the genie. But it will cost you twenty crores of rupees.”

“What?” the Minster retorts in a voice filled with pathos. “I am just a poor *brāhmaṇa*!”

“All right,” the old man mercifully replies, “nineteen crores.”

The minister could not believe his ears. Then he had an idea and offered his proposal: “I have a key to a guest room at ISKCON Pandharpur. You can become a life member and stay there for free!”

The old man’s eyes opened wide. His knees shook, and the world seemed to swim in a million directions around him.

In a soft voice, barely above a whisper, the old man began to sing, “On this day my entire wretched life has become successful. Residence in Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma . . . Residence in Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma . . . Residence in Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma . . .” The old man began to gently sway back and forth as visions of Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma appeared in his inner vision. Then he paused, contemplating the wonderful unconditional mercy of the Lord. Torrents of tears began to fall from his eyes. He began to speak again, in a faltering voice, gradually rising in a crescendo, until his voice was ringing throughout the market.

“Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma — the place of the wonderful Deities of Śrī Śrī Viṭṭhala-Rukmiṇī!

“Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma — the place where Lord Caitanya danced and chanted for eleven days!

“Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma — the place where Lord Caitanya fulfilled the external purpose of his South Indian *yātrā:* to ascertain the fate of His lost brother, Viśvarūpa!

“Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma — the place where Viśvarūpa entered *samādhi*!

“Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma — the place where Lord Nityānanda received initiation from Lakṣmīpati Tīrtha!

“Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma — the place of worship of Tukārāma Mahārāja!

“Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma — the place of the beautiful ISKCON temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Pandharināth and all Their wonderful devotees!”

The old man again spoke in a whisper: “I had desired wealth, but you have offered me the greatest of treasures: residence in Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma. I had desired broken pieces of glass, but you have bestowed upon me the most valuable of jewels. Therefore you are my greatest well-wisher and benefactor.”

The old man accepted the key to the ISKCON Pandharpur guest room with hands trembling with rheumatism and began to chant the names of the *iṣṭadeva* of Pandharpur, “Pandurāṅga, Pandurāṅga, Pandurāṅga.” Chanting these names again and again, the old man gently placed the bottle with the genie in the minister’s hands and then departed for Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma.

Of course, the clever minister and the king had no trouble at all with the genie, because they engaged him in continuous chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

In the same way, your servants here at ISKCON Pandharpur have no trouble at all with the genie of material existence, because you and your dear devotees like Lokanāth Swami and Janānanda Goswami have engaged all of us in constantly chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

We are sheltered in Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma under your lotus feet and the feet of all of our beloved *ācāryas*. By your mercy, we have received the greatest fortune in all of existence — residence at Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma. And for this we are always grateful to you and will endeavor to serve your mission in Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma until our last breath.

Thus, by reading your books, we have become attracted to Kṛṣṇa consciousness and have received the benediction of residence in Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma. Without your blessings, none of this would ever have been possible.

Your servants in ISKCON at Śrī Pandharpur-dhāma, India.  
(written by Ekalavya Dāsa)

## Paris

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

By your mercy and the help and blessings of the devotees, especially His Holiness Paraṁ Gati Swami and his Holiness Śivarāma Swami, as well as a group of elder and dedicated devotees from France and other places in Europe, particularly the Karuṇā Bhavan Temple in Scotland, we are now running a new preaching center within the Paris city limits.

For one year we have all been experiencing the miracle of going back on the streets of Paris. We do *harināma* every day and also some book distribution and *prasādam* distribution, and we invite people for the evening program. This is so nice! All the devotees in France are very happy about this good news, and we are confident that these activities can be continued and increased. It is a new life for us — to come back to actively participating in your preaching mission, to inviting everybody to come back to Kṛṣṇa. We are all experiencing a lot of happiness doing it.

For one year many devotees have been involved in the preaching, taking the opportunity to do a lot of service together and experiencing many wonderful moments. Paris is famous for its materialistic culture and opulences and for its philosophers and writers who propagate bewildering ideas about the meaning of life. Most of the educated people are victims of these philosophers, and the people in general are very much lost in forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will never be able to thank you enough for what you did. You came to the West at an advanced age, at the risk of your life, to deliver us fallen souls by giving us the most valuable treasure, love of Kṛṣṇa. It is so nice to go out among the people with the treasure of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* and your unparalleled books.

Many people smile at us and sometimes dance with us and come to speak with us. They appreciate the *prasādam* and the books also. When we meet such nice people, we invite them to our preaching center. Last Saturday we went to the Eiffel Tower with visiting Nepalese and Swiss devotees, and we remembered when you would come there for your daily morning walks and make it into a holy place.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we ask for your blessings so that this program will continue and increase more and more. Thank you again and again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bringing us back to life in the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Your servants in Paris, France.  
(written by Gadādhara Priya Dāsa)

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to you on the occasion of your most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā!

In a year in which we have had certain impediments placed in developing the preaching here, we can reflect on the transcendence of your personality, how you established so many centers all over the world in spite of advanced age, being in a foreign country, no financial resources, unsympathetic family members, and unhelpful godbrothers.

Despite such daunting circumstances, ISKCON grew, and thus the complexities of managing the society increased, but you never retired. You simply adhered to fulfilling the desire of your spiritual master, thus setting the perfect example.

In this way time has not diminished your accomplishments but enhanced our appreciation of them.

Please continue to bestow your mercy on us so that we may never think that worldly impediments can interfere with your transcendental mission.

Your servants at ISKCON Perth, Australia.

## Porto Alegre

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You have been so kind to us, always engaging us in your service and Kṛṣṇa’s service. Therefore we will always be in your debt, an endless debt that we can only attempt to amortize by working hard every day to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the fallen souls of this age.

We are trying hard to fix up the building we own in our city. Then we will be able expand our preaching of Kṛṣṇa consciousness more and more, as you and your spiritual master desire. We are so anxious that this house be ready this year, the thirtieth anniversary of your departure.

In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (9.10.54) we find this passage:

\[Translation:\] When Lord Rāmacandra, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, was the king of this world, all bodily and mental suffering, disease, old age, bereavement, lamentation, distress, fear, and fatigue were completely absent. There was even no death for those who did not want it.

\[Purport:\] All these facilities existed because of Lord Rāmacandra’s presence as the king of the entire world. A similar situation could be introduced immediately, even in this age called Kali, the worst of all ages. It is said, *kali-kāle nāma-rūpe kṛṣṇa-avatāra:* Kṛṣṇa descends in this Kali-yuga in the form of His holy name — Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Rāma. If we chant offenselessly, Rāma and Kṛṣṇa are still present in this age. The kingdom of Rāma was immensely popular and beneficial, and the spreading of this Hare Kṛṣṇa movement can immediately introduce a similar situation, even in this Kali-yuga.

From this we can clearly see that if we spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness the blessings of Lord Rāmacandra’s kingdom will gradually be manifest even in this dark Age of Kali. This is all due to your causeless mercy, which we continue to receive through the medium of your eternal teachings.

Therefore, we are so thankful for everything you taught us and are still teaching us. When you left this world you bequeathed to us the great responsibility of continuing to propagate your teachings, and we sincerely and humbly pray that you will bless us so we can fulfill this responsibility more and more.

Your servants at ISKCON Porto Alegre, Brazil.

## Prabhupāda Manor

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet! All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We would like to thank you from the bottom of our hearts for giving us the shelter of your lotus feet. In a letter you have given us the blueprint of success in Kṛṣṇa consciousness:

You mentioned that your pathway has become filled with stumbling blocks, but there are no stumbling blocks, I can kick out all those stumbling blocks immediately, provided you accept my guidance. With one stroke of my kick I can kick out all stumbling blocks. \[Letter to Kṛṣṇa Dāsa, 9 September 1972\]

We know that the key to success is to give up our speculative “knowledge” and just accept your guidance. If you say Kṛṣṇa is a little blue boy who plays a flute, whose is head bedecked with a peacock feather, and has a three-curved stance, then we accept that. If you say we’ve never been to the moon, we don’t hesitate to agree. If you are critical of “science” as we know it today, then we are obliged to also criticize the scientists. Why are we so willing to go against the grain? Because Your Divine Grace has done something that so few are doing today. You are simply repeating the words of your spiritual master and the words of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. In the *Teachings of Lord Kapila, the Son of Devahūti* (Verse 20) it is stated:

*prasaṅgam ajaraṁ pāśam ātmanaḥ kavayo viduḥ*  
*sa eva sādhuṣu kṛto mokṣa-dvāram apāvṛtam*

**Every learned man knows very well that attachment for the material is the greatest entanglement of the spirit soul. But that same attachment, when applied to self-realized devotees, opens the door of liberation.**

In the purport of this verse you mention that “. . . desirelessness is not possible for a living entity. A living entity, by constitution, has the propensity to be attached to something.” Please let our attachment be to your instructions, and please bless us so we don’t take your instructions out of context. We are firmly convinced that you truly can kick out all stumbling blocks from our pathway, and we humbly ask you to guide us within our capacity. Please let our sentiment mature into pure love for Your Divine Grace and Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. We know that only through devotional service can this be done, so please engage us in your service. Thank you for loving us!

Your aspiring servants at Prabhupāda Manor, Cleveland, Ohio, USA.

## Pretoria

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Compassion Will See Us Through!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On reflecting on the achievements of our temple over the last year, I am ashamed to admit that we have failed you. Our book distribution is down; this has always been our strong point, and I must admit that I know and understand the reasons. We are working on this and are hopeful that your compassion and love will help our temple through these strange times.

There is, however, good news in that our congregation is growing and we are getting new and qualified devotees coming forward to assist. We can still say that our Sunday programs are probably the most cosmopolitan and our *kīrtanas* the most ecstatic in the South African *yātrā*. We still believe that you are quite proud of the feasts that we prepare in Pretoria for your Sunday Love Feast; we believe that we are maintaining your required standards.

You said that in order for us to properly understand you we have to study your books. We are endeavoring to do this, and the lively discussions that take place during our programs at the temple are remarkable. In the infancy of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we understand that to understand you we have to not only study your books very scrutinizingly but also advance spiritually. You are the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, a position no ordinary devotee can occupy. Please bless us all that we can listen well so that with the mirrors of our minds cleansed we can see and hear things as they are and revel in the auspicious and sublime philosophy and practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We have some very nice plans to expand the preaching in Pretoria. We pray to receive your compassionate glance so that we can not only do this but can also advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā we again dedicate ourselves to you and your ISKCON. Please bless us so that we can be of use to you and your pure disciples in ISKCON. By rendering some meaningful service, we can receive your overflowing compassion for all the struggling *sādhakas* in this world.

His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

The most unworthy servants of your servants at ISKCON Pretoria, South Africa.

## New Govardhana Hill (Puerto Rico)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your ISKCON!

On this day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the devotees here in Puerto Rico want to thank you for giving us the opportunity to serve you.

We can see Kali-yuga making people miserable. Still, they do not readily accept the help we can offer them. When you were physically present in this world, you attracted so many people with your purity. You said purity is the force, which means that we have to be much purer and advanced in Kṛṣṇa consciousness to achieve substantial results in preaching.

We have a vibrant community of devotees from different parts of the world living outside and inside the temple. Even though tied up with *gṛhastha* duties, they still try to preach. You showed the perfect example in this regard, Śrīla Prabhupāda: you outwardly remained in householder life while at every moment simply devising different plans to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your pastimes in householder life were just like those of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura or Śrīvāsa Ṭhākura.

Unfortunately, in Puerto Rico we are now beset by various problems and challenges. Thank you for letting us take part in your movement. By defending ISKCON, we all feel connected to you more intimately. Thus we feel we are becoming more qualified as Prabhupādānugas. As His Grace Vīrabāhu Prabhu wrote in a letter: “ISKCON is the only institution in which Śrīla Prabhupāda is the ultimate authority.” You have set up ISKCON to help you extensively propagate authoritative knowledge about the conditioned souls and their eternal loving relationship with Śrī Kṛṣṇa. The uniqueness of ISKCON in comparison to other religious institutions is that the Vedic literature you have provided us gives a detailed description not just of God and the soul, which all other religions also give to some extent, but also of matter, which no other religion gives.

Once again, thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us ISKCON, a society where we can actually serve God.

Your disciple and granddisciples at ISKCON Puerto Rico.

## Rāḍhadeśa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

This Vyāsa-pūjā day is the most auspicious occasion to remember your pastimes, which belong to the eternal reality. We wish you a very happy appearance day, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You never physically came to Rāḍhadeśa, which was established in 1980. However, you are undoubtedly the heart and soul of ISKCON Rāḍhadeśa. You visited the Benelux countries for the first time in 1969. You came a second time in 1972 and invited Their transcendental Lordships Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā-devī to manifest Themselves in Amsterdam, Holland. Rāḍhadeśa became Their new home in 1980, as well as the home of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha-Lalitā-Viśākhā and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

Attempting to follow in the footsteps of your servants, we organize Ratha-yātrā every summer in Amsterdam, Rotterdam, The Hague, and Antwerp. Each time, hundreds and hundreds of fortunate souls gather to share a wonderful moment by chanting the holy names, taking *mahā-prasādam*, hearing about the glories of the Lord and His pure devotee, etc. All this is due to the miracle of Kṛṣṇa consciousness that you so mercifully brought to the West. Shall we one day be fully aware of all that you did and do for the fallen individuals of this age?

Under your divine inspiration, we are trying to make Rāḍhadeśa a house in which everyone can live very peacefully, at Lord Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. The challenge is great, as Māyā is so strong and our resources very limited, on a material level. However, we know that on a spiritual level, and by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, everything becomes possible. Therefore, we constantly take inspiration from your holy books and your personal example. We are convinced that by our remaining faithful and loyal to you and obeying your sublime instructions, Rāḍhadeśa will go on developing nicely and so many wonderful things will happen. Already, thousands of new people come every year to participate in our tour program, shop in our boutique and bakery, and enjoy *prasādam* at the restaurant. Our guest house also makes it easier for guests and visiting devotees to attend the morning programs, relish Hare Kṛṣṇa festivals, and participate in seminars at Bhaktivedanta College. By your mercy many more souls can receive the opportunity to start their journey back home, back to Godhead.

By your divine mercy, Rāḍhadeśa has become well-known in your ISKCON society, and even to the conditioned souls in the Benelux. It is blooming like a beautiful lotus flower in spring. Members from the Indian congregation now visit regularly. After years of anticult persecution, Rāḍhadeśa may soon be recognized as an official bona fide religious institution in Belgium. Devotees from all around the world also come to Rāḍhadeśa to receive spiritual inspiration. All this enlivens the Rāḍhadeśa Vaiṣṇavas in their own spiritual commitment and gives them more strength to bring even more souls to Kṛṣṇa. Yes, all ability comes from surrendering day after day at your lotus feet. If we go on doing so, we know by your mercy that Kṛṣṇa will be very happy with our service, and as you have told us, to make Kṛṣṇa happy is the perfection of life. Hare Kṛṣṇa. *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your insignificant followers, the bhaktas at Rāḍhadeśa, Belgium.  
(written by Bhāva-sindhu Dāsa)

## Rio de Janeiro

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**When East Meets West**

In 1966, Śrīla Prabhupāda arrived in New York in the middle of the hippie movement. What he found was a society degraded by consumerism and the horrors of the Vietnam War. According to *Śrīla Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta,* the hippies were young people searching for something beyond common life, trying to find spiritual satisfaction. From a general point of view, being a hippie was a state of the soul in search of prime elements, of liberty, of movement, of ideology, of art, of culture, of oriental religions, of yoga, of fraternity, and of the full flowering of one’s being.

The hippies were against all the social values ever established by the American society. But the way they opposed these values wasn’t all that correct. The hippie movement was like a seed, lying dormant, wishing to sprout and then bloom into maturity, breaking pre-established values and hoping to find renewal. The hippies were all over the world and could easily be spotted, not only by their distinctive clothes but also by their state of consciousness and their behavior. These were the people who, either by chance or by destiny, would become the congregation that would participate in the offerings of *kīrtana* by Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami, who give them spiritual guidance.

It was into this scenario that Śrīla Prabhupāda arrived in America. We are aware of all the difficulties he faced in introducing the mission consigned to him by his spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta SarasvatīGosvāmī Prabhupāda. Among all Americans, the best audience Śrīla Prabhupāda found was among the hippies, those at society’s fringes. These youth were willing to help him because they sensed that he had come to transform their consciousness and bring forth the true society they had been yearning for. Therefore, as we contemplate Śrīla Prabhupāda’s saga of transforming the world, let us not forget the immeasurable importance of this “mother cell” of hippies.

From the seed of his pure love for Kṛṣṇa and his determination to carry out his guru’s order, Śrīla Prabhupāda brought forth the entire world of ISKCON. We are eternally grateful for his causeless mercy, which lets us be the fruit of his Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We are also thankful for the blessing of being able to sing the holy names of the Lord in this era of disagreement and hypocrisy. Śrīla Prabhupāda is alive in each of his followers, who have already fundamentally established his mission.

During the 1970s, book distribution increased to astounding levels. According to *Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta,* in 1974 ISKCON temples sold nearly 400,000 hardback books and about 4 million copies of *Back to Godhead* magazine. News of such achievements made Śrīla Prabhupāda “become energetic like a young man.” In Los Angeles Śrīla Prabhupāda told one of his disciples, “My books will be the law books for the next ten thousand years.”

The energy Śrīla Prabhupāda emanated continues to flow into the hearts of his followers in profusion. We can categorically affirm that this movement is not under our control but under Kṛṣṇa’s control. This is obvious to any sensible and sincere person. The spiritual energy that continues to electrify our earth is coming from Prabhupāda. We can feel it when we see his smile, when we sing together, or when we hear the *mahā-mantra* on a corner, in a store, at an event, from a good man, on a flute, from the new generation of devotees, or in a choir. Let us not forget, of course, about Kṛṣṇa on the Internet. Actually, we are little servants, thirsty for Śrīla Prabhupāda’s love and mercy and for our efforts to be successful.

Śrīla Prabhupāda brought all these things — the fragrance of the incense in the temples, the sound of the sacred conch alerting our souls, calling us back to him. It is very difficult to forget him. He pulses inside us, in each thought, in each action. When I hear the sweet sound of the instruments playing, I feel alive with the true essence of life. He brought us all these things and much more. He rescued us, giving us the chance to be born, to live, and to die in complete wisdom.

Just as Lord Kṛṣṇa is attracted by devotion, Śrīla Prabhupāda is as well. So simply serve Śrīla Prabhupāda, because we will never be able to repay our debt to him, a debt that is ours for having received the mercy of a pure devotee of the Lord. Śrīla Prabhupāda saved the Western world definitively, leaving the biggest legacy that a human being could ever leave, which is the supreme chant, the *mahā-mantra,* which allows our crying to be heard by Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda is and will always be my eternal guide, life after life.

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama, Hare Hare*

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil.  
(written by Rāga Bhūmi Devī Dāsī)

## Rome

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most prostrated and respectful obeisances. All glories to your divine lotus feet, and all glories to your ISKCON, which is the greatest blessing for this material world.

When you heard that a new temple had been opened in Rome, you said, “The happiness I am feeling now cannot be understood in the three worlds.” These words are now a balm for the hearts of the devotees here, and also a push to act in such a way that your happiness may continue to grow.

This temple of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Śacīnandana has passed through many difficult phases, but by your mercy it is still here — by your mercy Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Śacīnandana have come here and remained here to enchant our eyes. By your divine inspiration many changes took place in the last year: a successful lunch program replaced the restaurant, and new people are happily joining ISKCON Rome and wish to dedicate their lives to your mission. We are all enlivened and inspired to improve and add more services.

In your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.8.27 you write:

A factual devotee, or *mahātmā,* does not give anything material to anyone because he has already left all material assets. He can, however, deliver the supreme asset, namely, the Personality of Godhead, because He is the only property of a factual devotee.

From this passage we learned that your only property is Kṛṣṇa, and you delivered to us this supreme asset, Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. You gave us all that is needed to end the material miseries and enter the blissful boat of devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. What you left us to do is to take your instructions as our life and soul and try to the best of our capacities to give Kṛṣṇa to others in the same way you gave Him to us.

Your only desire was to inundate the world with Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and our desire is to please you by flooding Rome with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We know that your blessings are already there; it’s up to us if we want to contribute sincerely to spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And we want it, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We want to be part of Lord Caitanya’s movement. Please grant us your merciful glance so we can always maintain this desire and so we can also improve the quality of our service to the Vaiṣṇavas and to the innocent people. In this way Your Divine Grace and Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Śacīnandana may be pleased.

Your humble servants at the Rome yātrā, Italy.

## Salvador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and respectful obeisances at your lotus feet! All glories to you, the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness!

To glorify our founder-*ācārya* is not a small task. Mere words are not enough; actions are also necessary.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you exist on the most perfect stage of pure absorption in thought of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

You gave to us and all the world the answers to all our questions and doubts, the solutions for all our troubles, and the remedy for all our pains.

You touched our hearts with your melodious chanting of the *mahā-mantra,* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

You gave us the most valuable gift, the opportunity to associate with Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu by taking part in His *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Oh, dear master and friend! Through your example and instructions the Lord revealed to us the perfect understanding of the true objective of human existence, self-realization.

We must open the windows of our hearts and let the divine fragrance of love for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa enter. We must make our lives more transparent and truthful and tune our souls to your pure vision. Than our conflicts will be resolved, our sickness will be put to flight, and our crazy minds, which falsely identify with the body, will be infused with divine happiness. And we will be inspired to work hard to fulfill your mission and the mission of Lord Caitanya: to save all the conditioned souls and bring them back home, back to Godhead.

We are so limited in our ability to really express our feelings for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! We pray for the ability and courage to demonstrate these feelings through humble, loving, Kṛṣṇa conscious relationships with one another, and to warmly welcome all people in the future who will come together and work in cooperation with the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Glorifying a pure Vaiṣṇava is even better than glorifying the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Himself. So . . .

Let us all chant together:

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

The servants of your servants at ISKCON Salvador, Brazil.

## Seattle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which are our only shelter in this dismal world.

Because you so kindly revealed yourself in this place of miseries, we are now able to conceive of a happy world elsewhere. Because you were willing to give up all bodily comforts, delivering the message of Lord Caitanya in the West, you made us comfortable with the balm of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Because you took the trouble to patiently elevate confused young people to the platform of divine coherency, we are now working en masse to distribute the teachings. And what did we learn? That God is a person. God as a person means He receives the attention we would accord to a loved one a million times multiplied. The Supreme Person is to be acknowledged as He who is the foremost recipient of all our respect, all our attention, and indeed all our love. Through the message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* you taught us the celebration of the *personality* of God. And who is this person? God, you indicated, is a position, but the *name* of that one so addressed is Kṛṣṇa, the all-attractive one. He, the indwelling spirit of the universe, the indwelling spirit in the heart of every living entity, and the eternally adored Supreme Personality of Godhead, is *kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam*.

Where does Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa reside? You told us in Goloka Vṛndāvana, the gardens of Vraja. What does He do there? He, the ultimate flautist, herds the cows with His friends, roams along the banks of the Yamunā and up in the hills of Govardhana. Sometimes at Kusuma-sarovara and other *sthalīs*, He enacts romantic pastimes, and sometime later, at Kurukṣetra, He speaks the *Bhagavad-gītā*. Thus you personalized everything like handwritten *śāstra* on the leaf of a palm. Yes, you handed us so many books, among them the teachings in the English edition of the *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu,* and acquainted us with the finer points of *bhāgavata-śabda*. Indeed Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave us everything to proceed on the road to that Vraja. In addition, through the medium of the *Teachings of Lord Caitanya*, you handed us cymbals of brass to loudly declare to the suffering masses and ourselves the wonderful nectar of *bhakti-rasa*. Thus you gathered people in the *bhakti* camp, produced books, and established over one hundred and eight temples worldwide.

And where are these temples, one may ask? Just check the back of the famous magazine *Back to Godhead,* which you established in the nineteen forties, and glean lists of locations from Athens to Amsterdam, Melbourne to Miami, Vienna to Vṛndāvana, and from South America to the city in northwest Washington, Seattle.

We, who are standing here today before you, are the inhabitants of that Seattle *yātrā*. Our community was established in the late sixties in the University District. Later on we were in Capitol Hill, and then again we had an old church nearby. Almost seventeen years ago we moved to the Seattle suburb village of Sammamish. Up on the plateau, next to the water tower, with a towering view of the city, the mountains, and an immense sky, Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Nīla Mādhava, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and the world famous Lord Jagannātha, brother Balarāma, and sister Subhadrā blissfully reside. Harbored at the highlands, on a small wooded plot, attended by faithful cooks and *pūjārīs*, far from the city but close enough to drive to, Their Lordships happily grant us daily *darśana.* Many devotees, in fact, have bought houses and plots within walking distance of the temple, and many others — those who work for Microsoft and others — live within the nearby college towns and municipalities. This happy community, guided by senior disciples, thrives on hearing, chanting, praying, and dancing with the aim of pleasing our foremost well-wisher, Your Divine Grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

On the twenty-second of April, 2007, we performed the ground-breaking ceremony for the new temple and Vedic Cultural Center of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Nīla Mādhava. Everyone came with a mind free from anxiety and a happy heart, singing and dancing, offering *pūjā*, words of good luck, gifts, flowers, and endless amounts of *prasādam* in celebration of the soon-to-be-realized new palace for our beloved Lordships. Yes, there was a silver roof over the head of the congregation, to be paid off slowly in days, months, indeed some years to come, but the gold in our hearts is the sparkling love that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have kindled with but a glance from your lotuslike eyes, a smile from your lips, and endless love from your Vaikuṇṭha heart. You are bringing us back to the garden of Vṛndāvana.

Here we will grow flowers and plants to scent the air, to beautify the land, and to pick for the *pūjā*. But the real offering, you say, is not necessarily the fruit or flower of the land, piled high in baskets of grass, but the love one has for Them, the Lords of the heart, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Nīla Mādhava. How to achieve this love? Only by the grace of the spiritual master: *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*. By your mercy Śrīla Prabhupāda, we receive the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without your grace we cannot make any advancement. So we beg you, please Śrīla Gurudeva, may we remember you not only formally three times a day, but throughout all twenty-four hours may we beseech you for guidance and grace. May we successfully build a house for our beloved Ṭhākuras, harmoniously work as a team, be happy with this sacrifice, and may we proceed toward you, who reside eternally in the spiritual realm of *sac-cid-ānanda-vigraha*. *Jaya* Gurudeva, Śrīla Prabhupāda! May your mission live on and on!

The servants of your servants at ISKCON Seattle, Washington, USA.  
(written by Rādhikā Devī Dāsī)

## Silicon Valley

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer you our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

You spent your last days on earth in Vṛndāvana, translating *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and speaking about the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa into a dictating machine that your servants held near your lips as you lay fasting, too weak to lift your head. Despite these circumstances, the words you spoke even then were infused with the saffron mercy particles capable of revealing the spiritual realm to anyone who hears them. And because you took all pains to arrange for it, your translations and purports are, even now, available to the world in beautifully glowing sets of books.

Earlier in your life, while managing your international movement, writing thousands of letters, counseling scores of devotees, and opening hundreds of temples, you continued to write and publish your books. But even before coming to America, when you lived in Vṛndāvana, you spent your days writing, publishing, and distributing your *BTG* while preparing other books, like *The Light of the Bhagavat*, *Easy Journey to Other Planets,* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. Soon after arriving in America, you wrote and published *Teachings of Lord Caitanya, Kṛṣṇa* book, *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*, *The Nectar of Devotion*, the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* and many others. You kindly engaged all your disciples in helping you with this work, allowing them the special privileges of editing, illustrating, publishing, and selling your books.

Upon your final return to Vṛndāvana from London, shortly before your physical departure, you told the assembled devotees, “There is nothing new to be said. Whatever I had to say I have already said in my books. Now you must all try to understand it and continue with your endeavors. . . .” And around that same time you added, “If I depart, there is no cause for lamentation. I will always be with you through my books and my orders. I will always remain with you in that way.”

As the perfect representative of Vedavyāsa, you miraculously produced and left us your divine books with the simple request that we read and distribute them for our benefit and for the benefit of the entire world. In this way, you have connected us to Lord Caitanya, who personally gave the following instructions:

I order every man within this universe to accept this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and distribute it everywhere. I am the only gardener. If I do not distribute these fruits, what shall I do with them? How many fruits can I alone eat? By the transcendental desire of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, water has been sprinkled all over the tree, and thus there are innumerable fruits of love of Godhead. Distribute this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world. Let people eat these fruits and ultimately become free from old age and death. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā* 9.36–39\]

Lord Caitanya further evoked the missionary spirit in his disciple Sanātana Gosvāmī, saying:

*māyā-mugdha jīvera nāhi svataḥ kṛṣṇa-jñāna*  
*jīvere kṛpāya kailā kṛṣṇa veda-purāṇa*

**The forgetful living entities, or conditioned souls, have forgotten their relationship with the Supreme Lord, and they are engrossed in thinking of material activities. Just to transfer their thinking power to the spiritual sky, Kṛṣṇa-dvaipāyana Vyāsa has given a great number of Vedic literatures. \[*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta*****,** ***Madhya-līlā*** **20.122, as cited in** ***Bhagavad-gītā As It Is,*** **Introduction\]**

In other words, the conditioned souls in this material world cannot free themselves. The Lord and His devotees must intervene in their lives by introducing them to the Vedic knowledge. This knowledge is so valuable that Lord Caitanya compared it to a priceless treasure. Upon receiving the treasure of Vedic knowledge, He said, the poor conditioned soul becomes spiritually rich.

Seeing the urgency of propagating transcendental knowledge, the merciful Vedavyāsa gave us the *Mahābhārata*, the *Bhagavad-gītā*, the *Purāṇas,* and the ultimate treasure, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*.
